            h.Dispatch(); await W(0.7); await Snap("28_hauler_lifting");
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && h.State != Hauler.St.Departing; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            h.Position = new Vector2(700, Hub.LaneY); await Frame(h.Position, 1.2f); await Snap("28b_hauler_departing");
            h.Position = new Vector2(Hub.PortalPos.X - 3, Hub.LaneY);
            for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && h.State != Hauler.St.Away; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Frame(Hub.PortalPos, 1.2f); h.T = Economy.HaulerAway - 0.3;
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && h.State != Hauler.St.Arriving; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await W(0.25); await Snap("28c_hauler_arriving");
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && h.State != Hauler.St.Returning; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            h.Position = new Vector2(Hub.HaulerPad.X + 3, Hub.LaneY);
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && h.State != Hauler.St.Landing; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Frame(Hub.HaulerPad + new Vector2(0, -20), 2.0f); await Snap("28d_hauler_landing");
            await W(0.5); await Snap("28e_hauler_turning_while_landing");      // about half way down, half way round
            Me().Visible = true;
        }
        // the arena, last: the trip replaces the hub scene, so nothing may follow it that
        // holds an old reference. The boss, its death-beam telegraph, its shockwave telegraph.
        {
            H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Arena); await W(1.5);
            var boss = H.Boss; var ash = H.GetNode<PlayerShip>("Ship_1");
            ash.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(120, 560); ash.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; ash.Rotation = 0f;
            GetViewport().GetCamera2D().Zoom = new Vector2(0.62f, 0.62f); await W(1.5);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && !N<Telegraph>().Any(t => t.Line); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await W(1.0); await Snap("38_arena_beam_telegraph");
            if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss)) { H.Boss.TakeDamage(90); await W(0.09); await Snap("42_boss_bar_chunk"); }
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 16 && !N<Telegraph>().Any(t => !t.Line); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await W(0.9); await Snap("39_arena_shockwave_telegraph");
        }
        GD.Print("SWEEP DONE");          // the runner fails without it: a cut-off sweep must never pass as clean
        GetTree().Quit();
    }
}

//==============================================================================
// FILE: tools/smoketest/run.ps1
// 151 lines   sha256 4eed3fc767ae
//==============================================================================
# Windows port of run.sh -- headless runtime check: boots the REAL engine, drives real
# input, and runs a host and TWO guests (three players) against each other over localhost.
# Catches what a typecheck cannot: null nodes, bad RPC paths, stale peer ids, scene-change
# ordering.
#
#   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\smoketest\run.ps1 <godot mono win64 exe>
#
# Differences from the Linux original, all forced by the platform:
#   * Uses the _console.exe variant. The plain Godot .exe on Windows is a GUI-subsystem
#     binary and does not write to stdout, so every check would be invisible.
#   * user:// is %APPDATA%\Godot\app_userdata\, not ~/.local/share/godot/app_userdata/.
#   * Output is captured to a file and filtered afterwards rather than piped live through
#     grep. A killed run keeps whatever it had already written, which is what the
#     --line-buffered plumbing was protecting.
# SmokeTest.cs.txt itself needs no changes: it contains no POSIX paths.

param([string]$Godot)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

# -Godot is optional now: find-godot.ps1 resolves it from an explicit path, WARSHIPS_GODOT,
# local.config.ps1, or a search of the usual places.
if (-not $Godot -or -not (Test-Path $Godot)) {
  $Godot = & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..\find-godot.ps1') -Godot $Godot
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or -not $Godot) { exit 2 }
}
$Godot = (Resolve-Path $Godot).Path

# The GUI binary prints nothing; swap to the console build beside it.
if ($Godot -notmatch '_console\.exe$') {
  $c = $Godot -replace '\.exe$', '_console.exe'
  if (Test-Path $c) { $Godot = $c }
  else { Write-Host "NO CONSOLE BINARY beside $Godot -- headless output would be invisible."; exit 2 }
}

$nupkgs = Join-Path (Split-Path $Godot) 'GodotSharp\Tools\nupkgs'
if (-not (Test-Path $nupkgs)) { Write-Host "no nupkgs folder at $nupkgs"; exit 2 }
$src = (Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..\..')).Path
$W = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'warships_smoke'

# One fixed scratch folder, so two runs cannot overlap -- the second would delete the first's
# files mid-run. Say so plainly: the bare failure is a Remove-Item error on a .err file, which
# reads like a broken harness rather than "something else is already running".
if (Test-Path $W) {
  try { Remove-Item $W -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction Stop }
  catch {
    Write-Host "CANNOT CLEAR $W -- another smoke run is probably still going."
    Write-Host "These runs share one scratch folder and must be run one at a time."
    exit 2
  }
}
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $W -Force | Out-Null
# /.godot, bin and obj are build output; copying them just makes the import stale.
robocopy $src $W /E /XD .godot .git bin obj /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS /NP | Out-Null
Copy-Item (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'SmokeTest.cs.txt') (Join-Path $W 'scripts\_Test.cs') -Force

$pg = Join-Path $W 'project.godot'
$t = Get-Content $pg -Raw
$t = $t -replace '(?m)^Net="\*res://scripts/Net\.cs"', "`$0`n_Test=`"*res://scripts/_Test.cs`""
# Its own user:// folder: the test creates and DELETES characters, and must never
# touch real ones. Wiped each run so migration and first-run paths are exercised.
$t = $t -replace '(?m)^config/name="Warships"', 'config/name="WarshipsSmoke"'
Set-Content $pg $t -Encoding UTF8 -NoNewline

$check = Get-Content $pg -Raw
if ($check -notmatch 'config/name="WarshipsSmoke"') { Write-Host "could not isolate user data; refusing to run"; exit 2 }
if ($check -notmatch '_Test="\*res://scripts/_Test\.cs"') { Write-Host "could not register the test autoload; refusing to run"; exit 2 }

$ud = Join-Path $env:APPDATA 'Godot\app_userdata\WarshipsSmoke'
if (Test-Path $ud) { Remove-Item $ud -Recurse -Force }

@"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<configuration><packageSources><clear /><add key="godot" value="$nupkgs" /></packageSources></configuration>
"@ | Set-Content (Join-Path $W 'nuget.config') -Encoding UTF8

Push-Location $W
try {
  # a test that does not compile tests nothing: stop here, loudly
  $buildLog = Join-Path $W 'build.log'
  & dotnet build -v q -nologo *> $buildLog
  $buildFailed = ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0)
  $errs = Get-Content $buildLog | Select-String -Pattern ' error ' | ForEach-Object { ($_.Line -replace '.*[\\/]scripts[\\/]', '') } | Sort-Object -Unique
  if ($buildFailed -or $errs) {
    $errs | Select-Object -First 20 | ForEach-Object { Write-Host $_ }
    Write-Host "SMOKE TEST FAILED (the build failed)"; exit 1
  }
  Get-Content $buildLog | Select-String -Pattern 'Warning\(s\)|Error\(s\)' | ForEach-Object { Write-Host $_.Line.Trim() }

  # NOTE: PowerShell variable names are case-insensitive, so these cannot be $F/$N --
  # a `foreach ($f in ...)` elsewhere in this script silently overwrote the filter with
  # a file path, and the run died parsing it as a regex.
  $keepRe = 'PASS|FAIL|DONE|Exception|   at |ERROR: [^B]|^  [a-z]'
  # Expected engine chatter, not failures: allocator notes, and Godot's own report that
  # no UPnP router exists (the game falls back and says so).
  $dropRe = 'RID alloc|PagedAlloc|find any UPNPDevices'

  function Start-Run {
    param([string[]]$GodotArgs, [string]$Tag, [int]$TimeoutSec)
    $o = Join-Path $W "$Tag.out"; $e = Join-Path $W "$Tag.err"
    $p = Start-Process -FilePath $Godot -ArgumentList $GodotArgs -WorkingDirectory $W `
         -NoNewWindow -PassThru -RedirectStandardOutput $o -RedirectStandardError $e
    [pscustomobject]@{ Proc = $p; Tag = $Tag; Out = $o; Err = $e; Timeout = $TimeoutSec }
  }
  function Complete-Run {
    param($R, [string]$Prefix)
    if (-not $R.Proc.WaitForExit($R.Timeout * 1000)) {
      try { $R.Proc.Kill() } catch {}
      $R.Proc.WaitForExit(5000) | Out-Null
    }
    $lines = @()
    foreach ($stream in @($R.Out, $R.Err)) {
      if (Test-Path $stream) { $lines += Get-Content $stream -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
    }
    # -cmatch, not -match: PowerShell matches case-insensitively by default but grep -E
    # does not, and a case-blind "FAIL" also matches every run's own "fails=0" summary.
    $keep = $lines | Where-Object { $_ -cmatch $keepRe -and $_ -cnotmatch $dropRe } | ForEach-Object { "$Prefix $_" }
    $log = Join-Path $W "$($R.Tag).log"
    Set-Content $log ($keep -join "`n") -Encoding UTF8
    $keep
  }

  & $Godot --headless --import --path $W *> (Join-Path $W 'import.log')

  $all = @()
  # fixed 60 fps: identical frame timing every run, so the DPS checks are exact
  $all += Complete-Run (Start-Run @('--headless','--fixed-fps','60','--path',$W,'--','solo') 'solo' 1200) '[solo] '

  $host1 = Start-Run @('--headless','--path',$W,'--','host')   'host'   60
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
  $g2 = Start-Run @('--headless','--path',$W,'--','guest2') 'guest2' 60
  $g1 = Start-Run @('--headless','--path',$W,'--','guest')  'guest'  60
  $all += Complete-Run $g1    '[guest] '
  $all += Complete-Run $host1 '[host]  '
  $all += Complete-Run $g2    '[third] '

  # the dedicated two-player arena run: after the three-player run, on its own port
  $ah = Start-Run @('--headless','--path',$W,'--','ahost')  'ahost'  60
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
  $ag = Start-Run @('--headless','--path',$W,'--','aguest') 'aguest' 60
  $all += Complete-Run $ag '[aguest]'
  $all += Complete-Run $ah '[ahost] '

  $all | ForEach-Object { Write-Host $_ }
  $bad = @($all | Where-Object { $_ -cmatch 'FAIL|Exception|ERROR' }).Count
  $done = @($all | Where-Object { $_ -cmatch 'DONE' }).Count
  if ($bad -gt 0 -or $done -ne 6) {
    Write-Host "SMOKE TEST FAILED ($bad problems, $done/6 runs finished)"; exit 1
  }
  Write-Host "SMOKE TEST PASSED"
} finally { Pop-Location }

//==============================================================================
// FILE: tools/smoketest/run.sh
// 57 lines   sha256 568b677787fd
//==============================================================================
#!/bin/sh
# Headless runtime check: boots the REAL engine, drives real input, and runs a host
# and TWO guests (three players) against each other over localhost. Catches what a typecheck cannot:
# null nodes, bad RPC paths, stale peer ids, scene-change ordering.
#
#   sh tools/smoketest/run.sh /path/to/Godot_v4.7.2-stable_mono_linux.x86_64
#
# Needs the .NET 8 SDK. Works offline: the Godot.NET.Sdk packages come from the
# nupkgs folder that ships beside the editor binary.
set -e
G="$1"; [ -x "$G" ] || { echo "usage: run.sh <godot mono binary>"; exit 2; }
NUPKGS="$(dirname "$G")/GodotSharp/Tools/nupkgs"
SRC="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)"
W=/tmp/warships_smoke
rm -rf "$W"; mkdir -p "$W"; cp -r "$SRC"/. "$W"/; rm -rf "$W/.godot"
cp "$SRC/tools/smoketest/SmokeTest.cs.txt" "$W/scripts/_Test.cs"
sed -i 's#^Net="\*res://scripts/Net.cs"#&\n_Test="*res://scripts/_Test.cs"#' "$W/project.godot"
# Its own user:// folder: the test creates and DELETES characters, and must never
# touch real ones. Wiped each run so migration and first-run paths are exercised.
sed -i 's#^config/name="Warships"#config/name="WarshipsSmoke"#' "$W/project.godot"
grep -q 'config/name="WarshipsSmoke"' "$W/project.godot" || { echo "could not isolate user data; refusing to run"; exit 2; }
rm -rf "$HOME/.local/share/godot/app_userdata/WarshipsSmoke"
cat > "$W/nuget.config" <<X
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<configuration><packageSources><clear /><add key="godot" value="$NUPKGS" /></packageSources></configuration>
X
cd "$W"
# a test that does not compile tests nothing: stop here, loudly
if ! dotnet build -v q -nologo > build.log 2>&1 || grep -q " error " build.log; then
  grep -E " error " build.log | sed 's#.*/scripts/##' | sort -u | head -20
  echo "SMOKE TEST FAILED (the build failed)"; exit 1
fi
grep -E "Warning\(s\)|Error\(s\)" build.log
timeout 180 "$G" --headless --import --path . >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

F='PASS|FAIL|DONE|Exception|   at |ERROR: [^B]|^  [a-z]'
# Expected engine chatter, not failures: allocator notes, and Godot's own report that
# no UPnP router exists (true in a sandbox; the game falls back and says so).
N='RID alloc|PagedAlloc|find any UPNPDevices'
set +e
# fixed 60 fps: identical frame timing every run, so the DPS checks are exact
timeout 1200 "$G" --headless --fixed-fps 60 --path . -- solo 2>&1 | grep --line-buffered -E "$F" | grep --line-buffered -vE "$N" | sed -u 's/^/[solo]  /' > solo.log
(timeout 60 "$G" --headless --path . -- host 2>&1 | grep --line-buffered -E "$F" | grep --line-buffered -vE "$N" | sed -u 's/^/[host]  /' > host.log) &
sleep 0.5
(timeout 60 "$G" --headless --path . -- guest2 2>&1 | grep --line-buffered -E "$F" | grep --line-buffered -vE "$N" | sed -u 's/^/[third] /' > guest2.log) &
timeout 60 "$G" --headless --path . -- guest 2>&1 | grep --line-buffered -E "$F" | grep --line-buffered -vE "$N" | sed -u 's/^/[guest] /' > guest.log
wait
# the dedicated two-player arena run: after the three-player run, on its own port
(timeout 60 "$G" --headless --path . -- ahost 2>&1 | grep --line-buffered -E "$F" | grep --line-buffered -vE "$N" | sed -u 's/^/[ahost] /' > ahost.log) &
sleep 0.5
timeout 60 "$G" --headless --path . -- aguest 2>&1 | grep --line-buffered -E "$F" | grep --line-buffered -vE "$N" | sed -u 's/^/[aguest]/' > aguest.log
wait
cat solo.log host.log guest.log guest2.log ahost.log aguest.log
BAD=$(cat solo.log host.log guest.log guest2.log ahost.log aguest.log | grep -cE "FAIL|Exception|ERROR")
DONE=$(cat solo.log host.log guest.log guest2.log ahost.log aguest.log | grep -c "DONE")
if [ "$BAD" -gt 0 ] || [ "$DONE" -ne 6 ]; then echo "SMOKE TEST FAILED ($BAD problems, $DONE/6 runs finished)"; exit 1; fi
echo "SMOKE TEST PASSED"

//==============================================================================
// FILE: tools/smoketest/SmokeTest.cs.txt
// 1956 lines   sha256 800fc34e158b
//==============================================================================
// Headless smoke test. NOT game code: run.sh copies the project to a scratch folder,
// drops this in as scripts/_Test.cs, registers it as an autoload, and runs it.
// It pokes private methods by reflection, so a rename there breaks this first.
// Stored as .txt so neither Godot nor the typecheck ever compiles it in place.
using Godot;
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

public partial class _Test : Node
{
    int fails = 0;
    void Check(bool ok, string what) { GD.Print((ok ? "PASS " : "FAIL ") + what); if (!ok) fails++; }
    async Task Wait(double s) => await ToSignal(GetTree().CreateTimer(s), "timeout");
    Hub H => GetTree().CurrentScene as Hub;
    int Count<T>() where T : Node => Nodes<T>().Count();
    System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<T> Nodes<T>(Node n = null) where T : Node
    {
        n ??= GetTree().Root;
        foreach (var c in n.GetChildren()) { if (c is T t && !t.IsQueuedForDeletion()) yield return t; foreach (var x in Nodes<T>(c)) yield return x; }
    }
    void KeyDown(Key k) => Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = k, PhysicalKeycode = k, Pressed = true });
    void KeyUp(Key k)   => Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = k, PhysicalKeycode = k, Pressed = false });
    void Key(Key k) { KeyDown(k); KeyUp(k); }
    // events enter in WINDOW coordinates
    Vector2 Win(Vector2 canvas) => GetViewport().GetFinalTransform() * canvas;
    Vector2 WorldToWin(Vector2 w) => GetViewport().GetFinalTransform() * (GetViewport().GetCanvasTransform() * w);
    void Click(Vector2 canvas, MouseButton b = MouseButton.Left) { var at = Win(canvas);
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = true });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = false }); }
    void DoubleClick(Vector2 canvas) { var at = Win(canvas);
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = true });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = false });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = true, DoubleClick = true });
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at, ButtonIndex = MouseButton.Left, Pressed = false }); }
    void AimWorld(Vector2 w) { var at = WorldToWin(w); Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = at, GlobalPosition = at }); }
    object Priv(object o, string m, params object[] a) => o.GetType().GetMethod(m, BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).Invoke(o, a);

    public override async void _Ready()
    {
        string role = OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs().FirstOrDefault() ?? "solo";
        Settings.TestVolume = 0.05f; Settings.ApplyVolume();   // six windows, nobody listening
        await Wait(0.3);
        try { if (role == "solo") await QuitResetsSector(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"sector reset test threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        try { if (role == "solo") await Select(); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"select screen test threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn");
        await Wait(0.4);
        // An exception must end the run as a failure. Without this the async test
        // simply stopped, nothing called Quit, and the run idled to its time limit.
        try { if (role == "solo") await Solo(); else if (role is "ahost" or "aguest") await ArenaMp(role); else await Mp(role); }
        catch (Exception ex) { Check(false, $"test threw: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}"); GD.Print(ex.StackTrace); }
        GD.Print($"DONE {role} fails={fails}");
        // let the mixer release the loops first -- in REAL time: at a fixed frame rate
        // 0.2 s of game time can pass in milliseconds, before the mixer has cycled
        Music.I?.Silence(); await Wait(0.1); OS.DelayMsec(200); await Wait(0.1);
        GetTree().Quit(fails);
    }

    // Hub.Sector is static and only Hub.GoTo ever sets it, so quitting to the main menu FROM THE
    // ARENA used to leave it on Arena: the next session skipped BuildWorld and dropped the pilot
    // into an arena with no base, no economy and a boss. The menu now ends the trip, whichever
    // route reached it.
    async Task QuitResetsSector()
    {
        Hub.Sector = Hub.SectorKind.Arena;
        Yard.TripClock = 123; Yard.TripCredits = 456;      // as a quit from the arena would leave them
        Music.CombatZone = true;                            // and so would this
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://MainMenu.tscn");
        await Wait(0.4);
        Check(Hub.Sector == Hub.SectorKind.Home,
              $"reaching the main menu ends the trip: the next session starts at home, not in the arena ({Hub.Sector})");
        // Yard._Ready APPLIES the trip snapshot, so leaving one behind overwrote the next
        // session's economy with it -- a previous character's, even.
        Check(Yard.TripClock == 0 && Yard.TripCredits == 0,
              $"the trip snapshot is dropped too, so a fresh session is not paid an abandoned one ({Yard.TripClock:0}, {Yard.TripCredits:0})");
        // Hub sets CombatZone on the way in; nothing cleared it on the way out, so quitting from
        // the arena left the combat track playing over the menu.
        Check(!Music.CombatZone, "the combat zone flag is dropped too: the menu is not scored as a fight");
    }

    // ── character select ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async Task Select()
    {
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://CharacterSelect.tscn");
        await Wait(0.3);
        var sel = GetTree().CurrentScene as CharacterSelect;
        Check(sel != null, "select screen loads");
        var slots = Character.List();
        // start with one saved character, made the way a player would
        Character.NewBlank(); Character.Name = "OldTimer"; Character.Class = ShipClass.Carrier; Character.Save();
        await Wait(0.05);
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://CharacterSelect.tscn"); await Wait(0.3);
        sel = GetTree().CurrentScene as CharacterSelect;
        slots = Character.List();
        Check(slots.Count == 1 && slots[0].Name == "OldTimer", "a saved character is listed");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).Count(p => p.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Row_")) == 1, "one row shown");

        // Character.Save writes a temp file and renames it over the real one, so a second
        // instance sharing this user:// can never read a half-written character. Saving
        // straight onto the live path caused "ConfigFile parse error ... Unterminated
        // string" on roughly 1 smoke run in 3. Two consequences are observable from here:
        // a stray .tmp is not a character, and the next save consumes it.
        var tmpPath = $"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg.tmp";
        using (var stray = FileAccess.Open(tmpPath, FileAccess.ModeFlags.Write))
            stray.StoreString("[id]\nname=\"half written");
        Check(FileAccess.FileExists(tmpPath) && Character.List().Count == 1,
              "a half-written .tmp is never listed as a character");
        Character.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(!FileAccess.FileExists(tmpPath),
              "Save() goes through a temp file and renames it away, leaving none behind");
        // ...and it really RENAMED rather than falling back to writing over the live file. Both
        // paths leave the same thing on disk, so the check above passes either way; only this one
        // can tell. Windows' rename() refuses an existing destination -- Godot works around that,
        // but if it ever stopped, every save would quietly take the unsafe path.
        Check(Character.LastSaveRenamed,
              "the save was committed by the rename, not by the fallback that writes over the live file");
        // user://settings.cfg is written the same way and for the same reason: two instances share
        // one user://, so a rebind saved by one used to leave the other reading a torn file and
        // silently falling back to defaults.
        var setTmp = Settings.Path + ".tmp";
        using (var strayCfg = FileAccess.Open(setTmp, FileAccess.ModeFlags.Write))
            strayCfg.StoreString("[audio]\nvolume_idx=");
        Settings.Save(); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(!FileAccess.FileExists(setTmp) && Settings.LastSaveRenamed,
              "Settings.Save() goes through a temp file too, and commits it by the rename");

        // new character: cancel writes nothing
        Nodes<Button>(sel).First(b => b.Text == "NEW CHARACTER").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        var cc = Nodes<CharacterCreator>().First();
        Check(cc.IsNew, "NEW CHARACTER opens a creator in new mode");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0 && Character.List().Count == 1, "Esc cancels a new character without saving");

        // new character: create
        Nodes<Button>(sel).First(b => b.Text == "NEW CHARACTER").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        cc = Nodes<CharacterCreator>().First();
        var name = Nodes<LineEdit>(cc).First(); name.Text = "Ironside"; name.EmitSignal(LineEdit.SignalName.TextChanged, "Ironside");
        Nodes<Button>(cc).First(b => b.Text.StartsWith("BATTLESHIP")).EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.05);
        Nodes<Button>(cc).First(b => b.Text == "CREATE").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.2);
        slots = Character.List();
        Check(slots.Count == 2 && slots.Any(s => s.Name == "Ironside" && s.Class == ShipClass.Battleship), "CREATE saves a second character");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).Count(p => p.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Row_")) == 2, "list shows both");

        // delete: KEEP leaves it, DELETE removes the file
        var old = slots.First(s => s.Name == "OldTimer");
        var row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).First(p => p.Name == "Row_" + old.Id);
        Nodes<Button>(row).First(b => b.Name == "Delete").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        var dlg = Nodes<ConfirmationDialog>(sel).First();
        Check(dlg.Visible && dlg.DialogText.Contains("OldTimer"), "DELETE asks first, naming the character");
        dlg.GetCancelButton().EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Character.List().Count == 2, "KEEP deletes nothing");
        row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).First(p => p.Name == "Row_" + old.Id);
        Nodes<Button>(row).First(b => b.Name == "Delete").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.1);
        dlg.GetOkButton().EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
        Check(Character.List().Count == 1 && !FileAccess.FileExists($"{Character.Dir}/{old.Id}.cfg"), "confirmed DELETE removes the file");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).Count(p => p.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Row_")) == 1, "row gone from the list");

        // play
        var iron = Character.List().First();
        row = Nodes<PanelContainer>(sel).First(p => p.Name == "Row_" + iron.Id);
        Nodes<Button>(row).First(b => b.Name == "Play").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.4);
        Check(H != null && Character.Name == "Ironside", "PLAY enters the hub as that character");
    }

    // ── single player ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // MY ship on every peer: "Ship_1" is only right offline and on the host (id 1);
    // on a guest it is the HOST's ship.
    PlayerShip Me => Nodes<PlayerShip>().FirstOrDefault(x => x.Mine);
    // class changes go through the creator path now (there are no class hotkeys)
    async Task SetClass(ShipClass c) { Character.Class = c; Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity"); await Wait(0.1); }
    System.Collections.Generic.List<(Vector2 p, float age)> Smoke(Torpedo t) =>
        (System.Collections.Generic.List<(Vector2 p, float age)>)typeof(Torpedo).GetField("_smoke", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(t);
    Vector2 Scr(Vector2 w) => GetViewport().GetCanvasTransform() * w;
    async Task Park(Vector2 at, float rot = 0) { var s = Me; s.Position = at; s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; s.Rotation = rot; await Wait(2.5); s.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }

    // The base menu builds a tab's contents only while that tab is shown.
    void ShowTab(Node panel, string tab)
    {
        var tb = Nodes<Button>(panel).First(b => b.Name == "Tab_" + tab);
        if (!tb.ButtonPressed) tb.ButtonPressed = true;      // fires Toggled -> the tab is built
    }

    async Task Solo()
    {
        Check(H != null, "hub loaded");
        Check(Count<PlayerShip>() == 1 && Me != null, "one ship named Ship_1");
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0, "no creator on entry");
        // the ship menu is behind REFIT in the BASE menu now, and it costs 10%
        Key(global::Godot.Key.C); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0, "C no longer opens the ship menu");
        H.Yard.Ore = 1000; H.Yard.Salvage = 500; H.Yard.Credits = 200;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        var bp = Nodes<BasePanel>().FirstOrDefault();
        Check(bp != null, "B opens the base menu");
        // THE THEME HAS TO ACTUALLY REACH THE BUTTONS, and for the project's whole history it did
        // not: a theme set on the root window does not cross a CanvasLayer, every piece of UI here
        // hangs off one, and so every button in the game drew Godot's stock theme. It went unnoticed
        // because the stock theme is ALSO a dark rounded rectangle -- it took a deliberately red
        // stylebox and a pixel count to expose it. Compared with the palette as literals rather than
        // with Ui.Card, so this fails both when a subtree stops inheriting and when the look changes
        // by accident. Counting the buttons first: an empty list must not pass as "all themed".
        var btns = Nodes<Button>().ToList();
        var unthemed = btns.Where(b => (b.GetThemeStylebox("normal") as StyleBoxFlat) is not StyleBoxFlat sb
                                       || sb.BgColor != new Color(0.102f, 0.129f, 0.173f)
                                       || sb.BorderColor != new Color(0.184f, 0.227f, 0.302f)).ToList();
        Check(btns.Count >= 8 && unthemed.Count == 0,
              $"all {btns.Count} buttons take the game's theme, not Godot's" +
              (unthemed.Count > 0 ? $" -- {unthemed.Count} do not: {string.Join(", ", unthemed.Take(5).Select(b => b.GetParent()?.Name + "/" + b.Name))}" : ""));
        ShowTab(bp, "REFIT");
        var reset = Nodes<Button>(bp).First(b => b.Name == "Reset");
        // The docked hauler keeps loading from stock every frame, so amounts are read
        // immediately around each click (a button press runs synchronously).
        double o0 = H.Yard.Ore;
        reset.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 0 && H.Yard.Ore == o0, "first RESET click charges nothing and opens nothing");
        await Wait(0.05);
        Check(reset.Text.StartsWith("CLICK AGAIN"), "it asks for a second click");
        double o1 = H.Yard.Ore, s1 = H.Yard.Salvage, c1 = H.Yard.Credits;
        reset.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        Check(Count<CharacterCreator>() == 1 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Ore - o1 * 0.9) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Salvage - s1 * 0.9) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - c1 * 0.9) < 1e-9,
              "second click: exactly 10% of ore, salvage and credits taken; the ship menu opens");
        await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "the base menu steps aside for it");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "B does nothing while the ship menu owns the screen");
        foreach (var c in Nodes<CharacterCreator>()) c.Close();
        await Wait(0.1);

        // the network options are folded behind one button
        var netToggle = Nodes<Button>().First(b => b.Name == "NetToggle");
        var netOpts = Nodes<VBoxContainer>().First(b => b.Name == "NetOptions");
        Check(!netOpts.Visible && netToggle.Text.Contains("MULTIPLAYER") && netToggle.Text.Contains("offline"),
              $"network options start folded behind '{netToggle.Text}'");
        netToggle.ButtonPressed = true; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(netOpts.Visible, "the button unfolds them");
        Check(H.GetNodeOrNull("Stars") is CanvasLayer sky && sky.Layer <= -100, "background stars on a deep layer");
        Check(Nodes<PanelContainer>().Any(p => p.Name == "HudPanel"), "stats line sits on a panel");

        var le = Nodes<LineEdit>().First();
        le.GrabFocus(); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Hub.ControlsLocked, "controls locked while typing");
        Click(new Vector2(1200, 700)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(GetViewport().GuiGetFocusOwner() == null && !Hub.ControlsLocked, "click off releases focus");
        netToggle.ButtonPressed = false; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(!netOpts.Visible, "and folds them away again");

        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
        var ship = Me;

        // ── no class hotkeys; class-specific controls line ──
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Key2); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Me.Class == ShipClass.Battleship, "2 no longer changes class");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier); Key(global::Godot.Key.Key1); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Me.Class == ShipClass.Carrier, "1 no longer changes class");
        var help = (Label)H.GetType().GetField("_help", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H);
        Check(help.Text.StartsWith("CARRIER") && !help.Text.Contains("mouse aims"), "controls line is carrier-specific");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(help.Text.StartsWith("BATTLESHIP") && help.Text.Contains("mouse aims the main guns"), "controls line is battleship-specific");
        Check(Abilities.ControlsHint((ShipClass)99) == "placeholder", "an undefined class shows 'placeholder'");
        ship = Me;

        // the painted turrets are the moving ones: every turret is a cut-out sprite
        var hullSpr = ship.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First();
        Check(hullSpr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("battleship_hull.png") && Math.Abs(hullSpr.Texture.GetHeight() * hullSpr.Scale.Y - 224f) < 0.5f,
              "battleship is 224 u (40% larger) on the repaired hull (turrets cut out)");
        var tsprites = Nodes<Turret>(ship).Select(x => x.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().FirstOrDefault()).ToList();
        Check(tsprites.Count == 6 && tsprites.All(x => x != null)
              && tsprites.Count(x => x.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("turret_bs_main.png")) == 4
              && tsprites.Count(x => x.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("turret_bs_pd.png")) == 2,
              "four main + two PD turrets drawn from the ship's own painted turrets");
        // the fields: each one's nearest edge 1500 u from the base -- room for a blockade
        {
            var rocks = H.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().Where(x => x.Texture != null && x.Texture.ResourcePath.Contains("asteroid_")).ToList();
            float beltEdge = rocks.Min(x => x.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - x.Texture.GetWidth() * x.Scale.X * 0.34f);
            var wreck = H.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First(x => x.Texture != null && x.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("behemoth_wreck.png"));
            float wreckEdge = wreck.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - 340f;                 // its visible edge toward the base
            GD.Print($"  field edges from the base: belt {beltEdge:0} u, wreck {wreckEdge:0} u");
            Check(rocks.Count == 9 && Math.Abs(beltEdge - 1500) < 8 && Math.Abs(wreckEdge - 1500) < 8,
                  "the mining belt's and the wreck's nearest edges are both 1500 u from the base");
        }
        var tioS = H.GetNodeOrNull<Sprite2D>("TIO");
        Check(tioS != null && tioS.Position == Hub.TioPos && Math.Abs(tioS.Texture.GetHeight() * tioS.Scale.Y - Hub.TioHeight) < 0.5f,
              "the Threat Intelligence Operations building stands south-west of the base");
        var bomb0 = GD.Load<Texture2D>("res://wing_bomber.png");
        Check(Math.Abs(Wing.BomberLength - 37.5f * 0.75f) < 1e-4, "bombers are 25% smaller (28.1 u)");
        var baseSpr = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("Base");
        Check(baseSpr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("base_station.png") && Math.Abs(baseSpr.Scale.X - Hub.BaseScale) < 0.001f, "the base is the new station sprite");
        Check(ship.Position.Y - PlayerShip.Art[ship.Class].Length / 2 > Hub.BaseBottom, "a new ship spawns clear of the base");


        // ── the idle economy: layout ──
        var Y = H.Yard;
        var portal = H.GetNode<Node2D>("Portal");
        var hauler = Y.Hauler;
        Check(portal.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY && Hub.HaulerPad.Y == Hub.LaneY, "portal and the hauler's pad share one horizontal lane");
        var baseImg = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("Base").Texture.GetImage();
        // sample the art across the pad's centre row, wherever the image puts it
        int padRow = (int)(Hub.HaulerPad.Y / Hub.BaseScale + baseImg.GetHeight() / 2f);
        int padW = 0; for (int x = 0; x < baseImg.GetWidth(); x++) if (baseImg.GetPixel(x, padRow).A > 0.2f) padW++;
        Check(Math.Abs(padW * Hub.BaseScale - 140f) < 3f, $"the bottom pad is 140 u wide ({padW * Hub.BaseScale:0} u)");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && hauler.Position == Hub.HaulerPad && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.01f
              && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - Economy.HaulerLandedScale) < 1e-4, "hauler sits landed (65%) on the pad, facing the portal");
        Check(Hub.DummyPos.All(p => p.Y - 46 > Hub.BaseBottom + 150), "the dummies sit well below the haul lane");

        // ── miners and salvagers ──
        var miner = Y.Gatherers.First(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
        var salv = Y.Gatherers.First(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager);
        Check(Y.Gatherers.Count == 2 && miner.Hold == 100 && salv.Hold == 100 && miner.Rate == 2 && salv.Rate == 2, "one of each: 100 hold, 2 per second");
        for (int i = 0; i < 6000 && !(miner.Beaming && salv.Beaming); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(miner.Beaming && salv.Beaming, "both reach their work and start beaming");
        await Wait(0.2);
        var bolts = (System.Collections.IList)typeof(Gatherer).GetField("_bolts", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(salv);
        var nose = salv.ToGlobal(new Vector2(0, -Gatherer.Length * 0.45f)); var aim = (salv.BeamTo - nose).Angle();
        float cone = 0; foreach (Vector2[] bl in bolts) cone = Mathf.Max(cone, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(aim, (bl[^1] - nose).Angle())));
        Check(bolts.Count >= 2 && cone < 0.33f, $"salvager's beam is a narrow scan: {bolts.Count} arcs within {cone:0.00} rad of centre");
        Check(miner.BeamTo.DistanceTo(Y.WorkSpot(miner).target) < 0.01f, "miner's single beam holds on its asteroid");
        double mined0 = Y.TotalMined, salv0 = Y.TotalSalvaged;
        for (int i = 0; i < 12000 && (Y.TotalMined - mined0 < 99.9 || Y.TotalSalvaged - salv0 < 99.9); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(Y.TotalMined - mined0 >= 99.9 && Y.TotalSalvaged - salv0 >= 99.9, "a full hold (100) of each is unloaded through an arm");

        // ── upgrades, by tab ──
        Y.Credits = 1000;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        var panel = Nodes<BasePanel>().First();
        Button Buy(string id)
        {
            ShowTab(panel, Economy.ById(id).Tab);                  // an upgrade lives on its own tab
            return Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(panel).First(r => r.Name == "Up_" + id)).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
        }
        Check(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(panel).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Up_")) == 5, "MINERS tab: five upgrades (count, hold, engines, beam, hull)");
        Buy("miner_hold").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("miner_hold").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Y.Level("miner_hold") == 2 && Y.Credits == 775 && Math.Abs(miner.Hold - 120) < 1e-9, "+10%: 100 then 125 (x1.25); hold 120");
        Buy("mine_rate").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Math.Abs(miner.Rate - 2.2) < 1e-9, "mining beam +10%: 2.2/s");
        panel.ShowTab("SALVAGERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("salvager_speed").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Math.Abs(salv.Speed - 121) < 1e-9, "salvager engines +10%: 121 u/s");
        Y.Credits = 10; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Buy("salvage_rate").Disabled && !Y.TryBuy("salvage_rate"), "short of credits: BUY greyed out and refused");
        Y.Credits = 30000; panel.ShowTab("MINERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        double c0 = Y.Credits;
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) { Buy("miner_count").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.02); }   // one press too many
        Check(Y.Level("miner_count") == 4 && Math.Abs(c0 - Y.Credits - 6000) < 1e-6, "+1 miner x4: 400, 800, 1600, 3200 (the fifth press refused)");
        Check(Buy("miner_count").Disabled && Buy("miner_count").Text == "MAX" && !Y.TryBuy("miner_count"), "capped at 5 miners: MAX");
        panel.ShowTab("SALVAGERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { Buy("salvager_count").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.02); }
        await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Y.Gatherers.Count(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Miner) == 5 && Y.Gatherers.Count(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager) == 5, "five miners and five salvagers");
        panel.ShowTab("HAULER"); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("hauler_pods").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Y.Pods == 2 && Y.Capacity == 600, "HAULER tab: a second pod (500 cr): capacity 600");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);

        // ── the arms and the queue ──
        foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers) { g.State = Gatherer.St.Working; g.Cargo = g.Hold; }   // everyone home at once
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var occupants = Y.Arms.Select(a => a.Occupant).Where(o => o != null).ToList();
        Check(occupants.Count == 5 && occupants.Distinct().Count() == 5 && Y.QueueLength == 5, $"five arms taken, one ship each; five wait in line ({Y.QueueLength})");
        var head = Y.Gatherers.First(g => Y.QueueIndex(g) == 0);
        bool firstIsHead = false, northOk = true, oneEach = true; int unloadedSeen = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 9000 && (Y.QueueLength > 0 || Y.Gatherers.Any(g => g.State is Gatherer.St.Docking or Gatherer.St.Unloading or Gatherer.St.Queued)); i++)
        {
            if (!firstIsHead && Y.ArmOf(head) >= 0) firstIsHead = Y.QueueLength == 4;
            foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers.Where(g => g.State == Gatherer.St.Unloading))
            {
                var arm = Y.Arms[Y.ArmOf(g)]; var pad = Hub.BasePos + arm.Pad;
                northOk &= (g.Position - pad).Dot(arm.Open) > arm.Reach;       // on the bay's open side
                unloadedSeen++;
            }
            oneEach &= Y.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null) <= 5 && Y.Arms.Where(a => a.Occupant != null).Select(a => a.Occupant).Distinct().Count() == Y.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(firstIsHead, "the first in line takes the first arm that frees");
        Check(northOk && unloadedSeen > 0, "ships unload through each bay's open north face");
        Check(oneEach && Y.QueueLength == 0 && Y.Gatherers.All(g => g.Cargo == 0 || g.State is Gatherer.St.Working or Gatherer.St.Outbound), "the line clears; never two ships on one arm");

        // ── the hauler: dispatch rules, landing, the run ──
        // Ten ships are working by now. Send them all back out empty, so no delivery
        // lands in the stock while the hauler's rules are checked (a hold takes 50+ s).
        foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers) g.ResetToWork();
        hauler.ResetToPad();                // it may be mid-run from the earlier sections
        hauler.Cargo = 0; Y.Ore = 150; Y.Salvage = 0;
        await Wait(8.0);
        GD.Print($"  hauler: {hauler.State} cargo {hauler.Cargo:0} / pod {Y.PodSize:0} x {Y.Pods}; stock {Y.Ore:0}+{Y.Salvage:0}");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && !hauler.CanDispatch, "150 of 300 in the first pod: cannot dispatch");
        hauler.Dispatch(); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading, "dispatch refused until a pod is full");
        Y.Ore += 200; await Wait(10.0);
        var hud = Nodes<HaulerHud>().First(); var go = Nodes<Button>(hud).First(b => b.Name == "Dispatch");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && hauler.CanDispatch && hauler.FullPods == 1 && !go.Disabled,
              $"one pod full (350 of 600): the floating DISPATCH button lights ({go.Text})");
        go.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting && !hud.Visible, "DISPATCH: it lifts off (and the button goes)");
        float s0 = hauler.VisualScale; await Wait(Economy.HaulerLift + 0.1);
        Check(s0 < 0.7f && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - 1f) < 1e-3 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing, "lifting grows it from landed to flight size");
        bool flat = true, facing = true; int frames = 0;
        while (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing && frames++ < 6000)
        {
            flat &= hauler.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY; facing &= Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.001f;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(flat && facing, $"it moves perfectly flat along the lane, facing the portal ({frames} frames checked)");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging && hauler.AuraOn && Math.Abs(hauler.Position.X - Hub.PortalPos.X) < 0.6f, "at the portal: blue aura, shaking");
        double credits0 = Y.Credits;
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerCharge + 0.5);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Away && hauler.WarpedOut, "warped out through the portal");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerAway + 0.6);
        Check(Math.Abs(Y.Credits - credits0 - 350) < 1e-6 && !hauler.WarpedOut && hauler.LastSale == 350, "back after 30 s: its 350 sold for 350 credits");
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.State != Hauler.St.Returning; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        flat = true; frames = 0;
        while (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Returning && frames++ < 6000) { flat &= hauler.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(flat && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Landing && hauler.Position == Hub.HaulerPad, "back over its pad along the same flat lane");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerLand / 2);
        float midScale = hauler.VisualScale, midRot = hauler.Rotation;
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Landing && midScale > Economy.HaulerLandedScale + 0.05f && midScale < 0.98f
              && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(midRot, Hauler.West)) > 0.3f && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(midRot, Hauler.East)) > 0.3f,
              $"it turns WHILE it descends (half way: scale {midScale:0.00}, heading between west and east)");
        // half way round it points north: its nose, at that size, must clear the station (lowest point y ~ 110)
        Check(Hub.HaulerPad.Y - Hauler.Length * midScale / 2 > 110, "turning on the way down, it clears the station");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerLand / 2 + 0.2);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - Economy.HaulerLandedScale) < 1e-4
              && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.01f, "lands facing the portal, and loads");
        Y.Ore = 700; Y.Salvage = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting && hauler.Cargo == Y.Capacity, "completely full: it leaves by itself");

        var t = Nodes<Turret>(ship).First();
        ship.Rotation = 1.3f; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(t.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.ToGlobal(t.Offset)) < 0.5f, "turret on its mount after turning");
        Check(Count<HullHud>() == 1 && Count<AbilityBar>() == 1, "hull bar and ability bar on the HUD");

        // ── naval helm ──
        await Park(new Vector2(-1200, 1400));
        var p0 = ship.Position; float r0 = ship.Rotation;
        // dead in the water: A/D PIVOT the hull slowly (10 deg/s) -- and never strafe
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.A); await Wait(1.5); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.A); await Wait(0.2);
        float portTurn = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(r0, ship.Rotation));
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(1.5); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(0.2);
        Check(portTurn < -12f && portTurn > -18f && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(r0, ship.Rotation)) < Mathf.DegToRad(2f) && ship.Position.DistanceTo(p0) < 0.5f,
              $"dead in the water: A/D pivot the hull slowly ({portTurn:0.0} deg, then back) and never strafe");
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(4.0);
        var fwd = Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation);
        Check(ship.SpeedAhead > 0.95 * ship.Stats["max_speed"] && (ship.Position - p0).Normalized().Dot(fwd) > 0.999f, $"W drives ahead along the keel ({ship.SpeedAhead:0} u/s)");
        Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["max_speed"] - 104) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["turn_radius"] - 107) < 0.01, "battleship: 104 u/s top speed, 107 u turning radius");
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(10.0);                    // settle into the turning circle
        float ra = ship.Rotation; double v = ship.SpeedAhead; await Wait(1.0);
        float omega = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(ra, ship.Rotation));
        double radius = v / omega;
        var side = new Vector2(-Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation).Y, Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation).X);
        float drift = Mathf.Abs(ship.Velocity.Dot(side));
        GD.Print($"  turning: {v:0.0} u/s at {omega:0.000} rad/s = radius {radius:0.0} (sheet {ship.Stats["turn_radius"]:0}); sideways drift {drift:0.00} u/s");
        Check(Math.Abs(radius - ship.Stats["turn_radius"]) / ship.Stats["turn_radius"] < 0.05, "turning radius matches the sheet (±5%)");
        Check(drift < 3f, "the keel holds: almost no sideways drift in the turn");
        KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W);
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.S); await Wait(8.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.S);
        Check(ship.SpeedAhead < -10 && ship.SpeedAhead >= -ship.Stats["reverse_speed"] - 0.5, $"S drives astern, capped ({ship.SpeedAhead:0} u/s)");
        // turning in place: at rest (<= 5% of top speed) the rudder pivots the hull, 10 deg/s, no strafing
        {
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
            var pp0 = ship.Position; float pr0 = ship.Rotation;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(2.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D);
            float pivoted = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(pr0, ship.Rotation));
            Check(Math.Abs(pivoted - 20f) < 3f && ship.Position.DistanceTo(pp0) < 1f && ship.Velocity.Length() < 0.5f,
                  $"at rest, D pivots the hull on the spot at 10 deg/s ({pivoted:0.0} deg in 2 s), without sliding");
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation) * (float)ship.Stats["max_speed"] * 0.6f;   // well above 5%
            float pr1 = ship.Rotation;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D);
            float underWay = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(pr1, ship.Rotation));
            Check(underWay > 15f, $"under way it turns by the rudder as before, far faster ({underWay:0} deg in 1 s)");
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        }

        // ── the K window: tabs, stats, rebinding ──
        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 1, "K opens the ability & stats window");
        var win = Nodes<StatsWindow>().First();
        var labels = Nodes<Label>(win).Select(l => l.Text).ToList();
        Check(labels.Any(x => x.Contains("Main guns (4 barrels):  29.50 DPS")) && labels.Any(x => x.Contains("Turning radius")), "stats tab: gun DPS and helm stats");
        Check(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(win).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Key_")) == 11, "keys tab lists the battleship's 5 abilities + 6 open slots");
        Check(Enumerable.Range(1, 6).All(i => Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, $"open{i}") == global::Godot.Key.Key0 + i)
              && Abilities.For(ShipClass.Carrier).Count(a => a.Open) == 6 && Abilities.For((ShipClass)99).Length == 6,
              "six open hotkeys (1-6) on every class, including undefined ones");
        win.StartCapture("missile"); Key(global::Godot.Key.H); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "missile") == global::Godot.Key.H && Settings.Keys.ContainsKey("Battleship.missile"), "missile rebound to H and saved");
        win.StartCapture("missile"); Key(global::Godot.Key.G); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "missile") == global::Godot.Key.G && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.H,
              "binding a taken key (G, fire mode's) swaps the two");
        win.StartCapture("missile"); Key(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "missile") == global::Godot.Key.G, "a reserved key (W) is refused");
        win.StartCapture("missile"); Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "missile") == global::Godot.Key.G, "V is warp, a fixed key: refused too");
        win.StartCapture("pd"); Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 1 && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "pd") == global::Godot.Key.Q, "Esc cancels a capture without closing the window");
        Nodes<Button>(win).First(b => b.Name == "ResetKeys").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "missile") == global::Godot.Key.F && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.G, "reset restores defaults (fire mode on G)");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 0, "K closes it");

        // ── dummies ──
        var d1 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
        var d2 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("PracticeFighter1");            // where dummy 2 stood: two practice fighters
        var pf2 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("PracticeFighter2");
        var d3 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
        Check(Count<TargetDummy>() == 4 && new[] { d1.NetId, d2.NetId, pf2.NetId, d3.NetId }.Distinct().Count() == 4
              && d2.Fighter && pf2.Fighter && d2.HitRadius < 20f && !d1.Fighter && !d3.Fighter,
              "two dummies and two practice light fighters (small craft), all with distinct ids");

        // ── battleship main guns: cursor aim, hold Space, salvo == staggered ──
        var dummy = d1;
        await Park(dummy.Position + new Vector2(0, 600));
        AimWorld(dummy.Position); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.GetGlobalMousePosition().DistanceTo(dummy.Position) < 3f, "test cursor lands on the dummy");
        await Wait(2.5);
        var mainT = Nodes<Turret>(ship).First(x => !x.PointDefense);
        float aimErr = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(mainT.GlobalRotation, (dummy.Position - mainT.GlobalPosition).Angle()));
        Check(aimErr < 0.02f, $"main guns point at the cursor (off {aimErr:0.000} rad)");
        Check(Mathf.Abs(ship.Rotation) < 0.001f, "the hull does not turn toward the cursor");
        dummy.ResetMeter(); await Wait(1.0);
        Check(dummy.Total == 0, "no fire without Space");

        ship.Staggered = false; dummy.ResetMeter();
        // shells take ~1 s to arrive: wait for the last ones before reading the total
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(20.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(1.5);
        double salvo = dummy.Total; await Wait(1.2);
        ship.Staggered = true; dummy.ResetMeter();
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(20.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(1.5);
        double stag = dummy.Total;
        GD.Print($"  salvo {salvo:0.0} dmg / 20s;  staggered {stag:0.0} / 20s;  sheet {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.000} DPS");
        double want20 = ship.Stats.MainDps * 20, oneShot = ship.Stats["main_damage"];
        Check(Math.Abs(salvo - want20) <= 4 * oneShot + 0.01, $"salvo delivers the sheet's {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.0} DPS (±1 salvo)");
        Check(Math.Abs(stag - want20) <= oneShot + 0.01, $"staggered delivers the sheet's {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.0} DPS (±1 shot)");
        bool before = ship.Staggered; Key(global::Godot.Key.G); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.Staggered != before, "G toggles salvo / staggered");

        // ── the missile: one in the magazine; a selected target in range, or it refuses ──
        Torpedo Heavy() => Nodes<Torpedo>().FirstOrDefault(x => x.Heavy && Smoke(x).Count < 40);
        string SlotMsg() => AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "missile", H.Selected) is var st && st.Fail ? st.Line : null;
        Check(ship.Stats["missile_mag"] == 1 && ship.MissilesLoaded == 1, "the battleship starts with one missile");
        // refused: nothing selected
        // Esc only if something is selected: with nothing selected, Esc opens the Esc menu
        if (H.Selected != null) { Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05); }
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Heavy() == null && ship.MissilesLoaded == 1 && SlotMsg() == "NO TARGET", "no target: nothing fires, and the slot says NO TARGET");
        await Wait(PlayerShip.FailShow + 0.1);
        Check(SlotMsg() == null, "the message clears after a moment");
        // refused: a target beyond range
        Click(Scr(d1.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        var keep = ship.Position; ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, (float)ship.Stats["missile_range"] + 150f); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Heavy() == null && ship.MissilesLoaded == 1 && SlotMsg() == "OUT OF RANGE", "target out of range: nothing fires, and the slot says OUT OF RANGE");
        ship.Position = keep; ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.1);
        // fired: in range
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); d1.ResetMeter();
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        var m1 = Heavy();
        Check(ship.MissilesLoaded == 0 && m1 != null && m1.TargetId == d1.NetId && d1.Total == 0,
              "in range: F launches the missile at the target (magazine 0/1); nothing lands at once");
        // speed from one frame's step, skipping its first frame (it may not have moved yet)
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var mp1 = m1.GlobalPosition; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        float mspd = (m1.GlobalPosition - mp1).Length() * 60f;
        Check(Math.Abs(mspd - ship.Stats["missile_speed"]) < 0.5f && m1.TurnRate == (float)ship.Stats["missile_turn"], $"it is slow: {mspd:0.0} u/s");
        for (int i = 0; i < 900 && d1.Total == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 6.25) < 0.01, "it lands: 5 damage, x1.25 by the default chips (6.25)");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Heavy() == null, "empty magazine: F does nothing");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(ship.Reloading && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "reload", H.Selected).Busy > 0.9f, "R starts the reload; the bar shows it");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Heavy() == null, "nothing fires while reloading");
        await Wait(16.2);
        Check(!ship.Reloading && ship.MissilesLoaded == 1, "reload refills the magazine (1) after 16 s");
        // barely guided: move the target sideways and watch how fast the nose follows
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        var m2 = Heavy(); var dhome = d1.Position;
        d1.Position = dhome + new Vector2(260, 0);
        float a0 = m2.Dir.Angle(); await Wait(1.0);
        float turned = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(a0, m2.Dir.Angle()));
        GD.Print($"  missile turned {turned:0.000} rad in 1 s toward a target that moved (limit {ship.Stats["missile_turn"]:0.00})");
        Check(turned > 0.05 && turned <= ship.Stats["missile_turn"] + 0.01, "it steers toward the target, but no faster than 0.35 rad/s");
        await Wait(6.0); d1.Position = dhome;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(16.2);                  // leave it loaded for what follows

        // ── the main guns fire SHELLS: straight, 4x the missile's speed, damage on arrival ──
        {
            var gs = Me; var gd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            var gKeep = gs.Position; gs.Position = gd.Position + new Vector2(0, 520); gs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; gs.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.6);
            AimWorld(gd.Position); await Wait(1.2);                                   // turrets swing onto it
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); gd.ResetMeter();
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, Pressed = true });
            Shell sh1 = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && sh1 == null; i++) { AimWorld(gd.Position); sh1 = Nodes<Shell>().FirstOrDefault(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, Pressed = false });
            double atFire = gd.Total;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var sp0 = sh1.Position; var sd0 = sh1.Dir;
            for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float shSpd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(sh1) ? (sh1.Position - sp0).Length() / (10f / 60f) : 0f;
            Check(Math.Abs(shSpd - 4 * (float)gs.Stats["missile_speed"]) < 6f && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(sh1) && sh1.Dir == sd0 && atFire == 0,
                  $"the guns fire shells at 4x the missile's speed ({shSpd:0} u/s), straight, and nothing lands at once");
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && gd.Total == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(gd.Total > 0 && Math.Abs(gd.Total % gs.Stats["main_damage"]) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(gs.Stats["main_damage"] - 7.375) < 1e-9, $"a shell lands for 5.9 x1.25 by the chips = 7.375 ({gd.Total:0.000})");
            // the balance: the battleship's total is 1.25x the carrier's measured 19.07 DPS; the carrier reaches 1.5x as far
            var bsS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship); var caS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier);
            double bsTotal = bsS.MainDps + bsS.MissileDps;
            Check(Math.Abs(bsTotal / 19.07 - 1.25) < 0.02, $"the battleship's damage ({bsTotal:0.00} DPS) is 1.25x the carrier's measured 19.07");
            Check(Math.Abs(caS["control_range"] - 1.5 * bsS["main_range"]) < 1e-6, "the carrier reaches 1.5x as far as the battleship's guns (1080 u)");
            gs.Position = gKeep; gs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        }

        // ── warp (V): every capital ship; a hull cooldown, not a slot ──
        {
            var me = Me; var warpHome = me.Position; var d2w = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
            void Ready() { typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_warpCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Rotation = 0f; }
            void Deselect() => typeof(Hub).GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
            Check(Abilities.For(me.Class).All(a => a.Id != "warp"), "warp takes no slot on the ability bar");
            Ready(); Deselect(); var start = me.Position;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(me.Warping && me.Position.DistanceTo(start) < 20f, "V starts a 3 s warm-up; the ship holds until it jumps");
            await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup);
            var moved = me.Position - start;
            Check(!me.Warping && Math.Abs(moved.Length() - 2000f) < 40f && Mathf.Abs(Vector2.Up.AngleTo(moved)) < 0.05f,
                  $"nothing selected ahead: 2000 u straight ahead ({moved.Length():0} u)");
            Check(Math.Abs(me.WarpCooldownLeft - PlayerShip.WarpCooldown) < 0.6 && PlayerShip.WarpCooldown == 30, "then a 30 s cooldown");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!me.Warping, "V does nothing while it cools down");
            // a target within 45 degrees of the bow: arrive just short of it
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); await Wait(0.3);
            Click(Scr(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            float want = d2w.HitRadius + PlayerShip.Art[me.Class].Length * 0.5f + PlayerShip.WarpStandoff;
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) - want) < 20f, $"a target ahead: it warps to just short of it ({me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position):0} u, wanted {want:0})");
            // a target behind: outside the cone, so straight ahead instead
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, -900); var s3 = me.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Position - s3).Y + 2000f) < 40f && H.Selected == d2w, "a target behind (outside 45 degrees): straight ahead instead");
            // aimed when it JUMPS, by the heading then: swing onto a target during the charge...
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, -900); await Wait(0.2);          // facing north, d2 behind
            Click(Scr(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(1.0);
            me.Rotation = Mathf.Pi; await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup - 1.0 + 0.2);                // now facing it
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) - want) < 20f, "V pressed facing away, then turned onto the target: it warps to the target");
            // ...or turn away from it, and it goes straight on along the new heading
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); await Wait(0.2);           // facing north, d2 ahead
            var s4 = me.Position;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(1.0);
            me.Rotation = Mathf.Pi / 2f; await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup - 1.0 + 0.2);            // now facing east
            var mv = me.Position - s4;
            Check(Math.Abs(mv.X - 2000f) < 40f && Math.Abs(mv.Y) < 40f, $"V pressed facing the target, then turned east: 2000 u east ({mv.X:0}, {mv.Y:0})");
            // the radar: click a marker to select it -- an enemy as the target, a landmark as a waypoint
            Ready(); me.Position = warpHome + new Vector2(0, 300); me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.6);
            var radar = Nodes<Radar>().First(); var rr = radar.GetGlobalRect(); float rad = rr.Size.X / 2f;
            Vector2 OnRadar(Vector2 w) => rr.Position + new Vector2(rad, rad) + (w - me.ViewPosition) * ((rad - 6f) / Radar.Range);
            H.ClearSelection();
            Click(OnRadar(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == d2w, "clicking an enemy on the radar makes it the target");
            Click(OnRadar(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == null && H.Waypoint == Hub.BasePos && H.WaypointName == "BASE", "clicking the base on the radar makes it a waypoint (and drops the target)");
            me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 1500); me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.2);    // the base dead ahead
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            float wantB = 260f + PlayerShip.Art[me.Class].Length * 0.5f + PlayerShip.WarpStandoff;
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - wantB) < 20f, $"a warp to the waypoint lands just short of the base ({me.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} u)");
            H.ClearSelection();
            Ready(); me.Position = warpHome;
        }

        // ── battleship PD: idle until Q; slow independent turrets ──
        await Park(new Vector2(780, 700), 0);                      // among all three dummies
        foreach (var d in Nodes<TargetDummy>()) d.ResetMeter();
        await Wait(2.0);
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) == 0 && !ship.PdActive, "PD does nothing until activated");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(4.0);
        var pds = ship.PdTurrets;
        Check(ship.PdActive && pds.All(x => x.Target != null) && pds.Select(x => x.Target).Distinct().Count() == pds.Count,
              $"Q: each battleship PD turret picks its own target ({string.Join(",", pds.Select(x => (x.Target as TargetDummy)?.Number))})");
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) > 0, "PD fires while active");
        Check(ship.Stats["pd_turn"] < Mathf.Tau / 2.9f, "battleship PD turns slowly (τ/3)");
        await Wait(11.5);
        Check(!ship.PdActive && ship.PdRechargeLeft > 14, "the window closes after 15 s and recharge starts");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(!ship.PdActive, "Q does nothing while recharging");
        double mid = Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total); await Wait(2.0);
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) == mid, "no PD fire while recharging");
        // A readout goes to the dummy with that NUMBER, not that position. The dummies are 1, 3,
        // 4 and 5 (2's spot holds the two practice fighters), so indexing the list by number-1
        // sent 3's figures to 4, 4's to 5, and dropped 5's entirely because 5 > Count: guests
        // read the wrong numbers off the wrong hulls.
        var dummy5 = Nodes<TargetDummy>().First(x => x.Number == 5);
        var dummy4 = Nodes<TargetDummy>().First(x => x.Number == 4);
        double l5 = dummy5.LastSecond, a5 = dummy5.Average10, t5 = dummy5.Total;
        double l4 = dummy4.LastSecond, a4 = dummy4.Average10, t4 = dummy4.Total;
        Priv(H, "NetDummy", 5, 7.0, 8.0, 9.0);
        double got5 = dummy5.Total, got4 = dummy4.Total;                   // read BEFORE restoring
        bool routed = Math.Abs(got5 - 9.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(got4 - t4) < 1e-9;
        dummy5.SetReadout(l5, a5, t5); dummy4.SetReadout(l4, a4, t4);      // put them back
        Check(routed, $"a dummy's readout reaches the dummy with that NUMBER, not that position (5 got {got5:0.#} of 9, 4 sat at {got4:0.#})");
        // Combat.Clear drops the hooks the world installed on its way out. OnShell was left
        // behind: a lambda holding the Hub, so the next shot called into a freed node, and a Hub
        // that could never be collected once you were back at the menu. Snapshot and restore --
        // this all happens inside one frame, so nothing ever sees the cleared state.
        var keepHostiles = Combat.Hostiles.ToList(); var keepPlayers = Combat.Players.ToList();
        var keepFlash = Combat.OnFlash; var keepShell = Combat.OnShell; var keepTorp = Combat.OnTorpedo;
        Combat.Clear();
        bool hooksDropped = Combat.OnFlash == null && Combat.OnShell == null && Combat.OnTorpedo == null;
        Combat.Hostiles.AddRange(keepHostiles); Combat.Players.AddRange(keepPlayers);
        Combat.OnFlash = keepFlash; Combat.OnShell = keepShell; Combat.OnTorpedo = keepTorp;
        Check(hooksDropped, "Combat.Clear drops every hook the world installed, OnShell included");

        // ── carrier ──
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier); ship = Me;
        Check(ship.PdTurrets.Count == 3, "carrier has three PD turrets, one per painted dome");

        // bombers dock on the flanks, half each side, and stay put as the carrier moves
        Wing[] Bombers() => Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.IsBomber).ToArray();
        async Task CheckDocks(int n)
        {
            await Wait(0.3);
            var bs = Bombers();
            int port = bs.Count(w => ship.ToLocal(w.GlobalPosition).X < 0), stbd = bs.Count(w => ship.ToLocal(w.GlobalPosition).X > 0);
            Check(bs.Length == n && bs.All(w => w.Docked && w.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.DockSlot(w).pos) < 0.5f) && port == n / 2 + n % 2 && stbd == n / 2,
                  $"{n} bombers docked: {port} port, {stbd} starboard");
        }
        await CheckDocks(2);
        Character.Bonuses["bomber_count"] = 2.0; ship.SetClass(ship.Class); ship = Me;   // 2 x (1 + 2) = 6
        await CheckDocks(6);
        Character.Bonuses.Remove("bomber_count"); ship.SetClass(ship.Class); ship = Me;
        ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["control_range"] - 1080) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["strike_range"] - 2160) < 0.01,
              "fighter control range 1080 (1.5x the battleship's guns); bomber strike range 2160 (2x)");
        var spr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(ship).First();
        Check(spr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("carrier_player.png") && Math.Abs(spr.Texture.GetHeight() * spr.Scale.Y - 170f) < 0.5f,
              "carrier flies the new symmetrical sprite at 170 u");
        await Park(new Vector2(780, 700), 0);
        var pdLight = H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(700, 860));            // a third light target (the practice fighters are two)
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(2.0);
        var cpds = ship.PdTurrets;
        var pdts = cpds.Select(x => x.Target).Where(x => x != null).ToList();
        Check(pdts.Distinct().Count() == 3 && pdts.All(t => t is Raider || (t is TargetDummy td && td.Fighter)),
              "carrier PD: three turrets on three different LIGHT targets -- never a plain dummy");
        // two light targets for three turrets: the spare must double up on a light one, never a dummy
        if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(pdLight)) pdLight.TakeDamage(9999);
        await Wait(1.5);
        var pdts2 = cpds.Select(x => x.Target).Where(x => x != null).ToList();
        Check(pdts2.Count > 0 && pdts2.All(t => t is Raider || (t is TargetDummy td2 && td2.Fighter)),
              "with more turrets than light targets, the spare turret still never takes a plain dummy");

        // clicks select only; Space attacks; R recalls
        await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300));
        Click(Scr(d3.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d3, "carrier: left-click selects dummy 3");
        Click(Scr(d3.Position), MouseButton.Right); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == null && ship.StrikeTarget == null, "carrier: clicks never order an attack");
        Check(Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter).All(w => w.Inside && !w.Visible),
              "idle fighters wait docked inside the carrier, out of sight");
        foreach (var bw in Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.IsBomber && w.Docked))
        {
            bool port = ship.ToLocal(bw.Position).X < 0;
            float want = ship.Rotation + (port ? -Mathf.Pi / 2f : Mathf.Pi / 2f);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(bw.Rotation, want)) < 0.05f && Mathf.Abs(ship.ToLocal(bw.Position).X) > PlayerShip.Art[ShipClass.Carrier].HalfWidth + Wing.BomberLength * 0.4f,
                  $"docked bomber faces out from the {(port ? "port" : "starboard")} side, tail to the hull");
        }
        int sig0 = ship.SignalsLit;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == d3 && ship.StrikeTarget == null, "Space sends the fighters only (bombers are their own ability)");
        // fighters leave one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart (a hard limit, not a stat)
        var fts = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter).ToList();
        for (int fr = 0; fr < 200 && fts.Any(w => w.LaunchedAt < 0); fr++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var order = fts.Select(w => w.LaunchedAt).Where(t => t >= 0).OrderBy(t => t).ToList();
        double minGap = 99; for (int g = 1; g < order.Count; g++) minGap = Math.Min(minGap, order[g] - order[g - 1]);
        GD.Print($"  fighter launches at {string.Join(", ", order.Select(t => t.ToString("0.000")))} s; smallest gap {minGap:0.000} s");
        Check(fts.Count == 3 && ship.Stats["fighter_count"] == 3, "the carrier starts with 3 fighters");
        Check(order.Count == 3 && minGap >= Wing.LaunchInterval - 1e-9, "fighters launch one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart");
        Check(ship.SignalsLit > sig0 || order.Count == 3, "launching fighters light the hangar signal");
        Check(ship.Stats["torpedo_speed"] == 90 && ship.Stats["torpedo_interval"] == 0.5, "torpedoes: 90 u/s (25% slower), one every 0.5 s (70% of the old rate)");
        var fx0 = H.GetChildren().OfType<FlashLayer>().FirstOrDefault();
        Check(fx0 != null && !fx0.ZAsRelative && fx0.ZIndex > ship.ZIndex && fx0.ZIndex < ship.ZIndex + Nodes<Turret>(ship).First().ZIndex,
              "hit flashes draw above the hulls and below the turrets");
        // strafing runs: 3 shots, straight through by 1.2x the target's diameter, then round again
        var fx = Nodes<Wing>().First(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter);
        int passes = 0; bool three = true, through = true; string lastPhase = fx.FighterPhase; float worst = 999f;
        for (int i = 0; i < 780; i++)                     // 13 s, inside the first 15 s of firing
        {
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            string ph = fx.FighterPhase;
            if (lastPhase == "Burst" && ph == "Overshoot") { passes++; three &= fx.ShotsThisPass == Wing.BurstShots; }
            if (lastPhase == "Overshoot" && ph == "Turn") { through &= fx.LastOvershoot >= Wing.Overshoot * 2f * d3.HitRadius - 0.5f; worst = Mathf.Min(worst, fx.LastOvershoot); }
            lastPhase = ph;
        }
        GD.Print($"  fighter: {passes} passes in 13 s; shortest overshoot {worst:0} u (needs {Wing.Overshoot * 2f * d3.HitRadius:0})");
        Check(passes >= 2 && three, $"fighters strafe: every pass fires exactly 3 shots ({passes} passes)");
        Check(through, "each pass carries on through the target by at least 1.2x its diameter, then turns");
        var fighters = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.IsBomber).ToList();
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && !fighters.All(f => f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => f.Resting), "after 15 s of firing, every fighter heads home to rest");
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && !fighters.All(f => f.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.GlobalPosition) < 1f); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => f.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.GlobalPosition) < 1f), "they rest at the centre of the carrier");
        d3.ResetMeter(); await Wait(2.0);
        Check(d3.Total == 0, "no fire while resting");
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && fighters.Any(f => f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => !f.Resting), "after 3 s they rejoin the fight");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == null, "R recalls the fighters");
        await Wait(3.0); d3.ResetMeter(); await Wait(1.0);
        Check(d3.Total == 0, "recalled fighters stop firing");

        // ── bombers: an active ability; straight, steady torpedoes with smoke ──
