        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5);
        Click(Scr(d1.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        // beyond the strike range the order is refused
        var near1 = ship.Position;
        ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 2900); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.StrikeTarget == null && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line == "OUT OF RANGE",
              "a target beyond 2x control range: strike refused, slot says OUT OF RANGE");
        ship.Position = near1; ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.StrikeTarget == d1, "F sends the bombers at the target");
        Wing crawl = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 2400 && crawl == null; i++) { crawl = Nodes<Wing>().FirstOrDefault(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Launching); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(crawl != null && crawl.Velocity.Length() > 5f && crawl.Velocity.Dot(d1.Position - crawl.Position) > 0,
              $"bombers keep closing slowly while they launch ({crawl?.Velocity.Length():0} u/s)");
        var fspr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(Nodes<Wing>().First(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.IsBomber)).First(); var bspr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(crawl).First();
        Check(Math.Abs(fspr.Texture.GetHeight() * fspr.Scale.Y - 17f) < 0.1f && Math.Abs(bspr.Texture.GetHeight() * bspr.Scale.Y - 28.125f) < 0.1f, "fighters 17 u, bombers 28.1 u (snout-nosed, 25% smaller)");
        Torpedo first = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && first == null; i++) { first = Nodes<Torpedo>().FirstOrDefault(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(first != null, "bombers launch torpedoes");
        // measure over an exact frame count: timers round up to the next frame
        var dir0 = first.Dir; var q0 = first.GlobalPosition;
        for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        float spd = (first.GlobalPosition - q0).Length() / (30f / 60f);
        Check(first.Dir == dir0 && Math.Abs(spd - ship.Stats["torpedo_speed"]) < 2, $"torpedo runs straight at a steady {spd:0} u/s (no tracking)");
        Check(Smoke(first).Count > 3, "torpedo leaves a smoke trail");
        await Wait(4.0);
        Check(d1.Total > 0 && Math.Abs(d1.Total % ship.Stats["torpedo_damage"]) < 0.01, $"torpedoes hit for 3 each ({d1.Total:0})");
        Check(AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line.StartsWith("REARM") || AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line == "RETURNING",
              $"bomber slot shows the return / rearm ({AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line})");
        await Wait(15.0);
        Check(ship.BombersReady == 2 && Bombers().All(w => w.Docked), "bombers return to their docks, rearm, and are ready again");
        // the docking fix: bombers dock and re-arm even while the carrier TURNS hard (a turning
        // carrier swings their slots sideways; they used to hover beside them, never docking)
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !Bombers().All(w => w.Ammo <= 0 || !w.Docked); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        // FULL SPEED is what trapped them: the old code moved a bomber to its slot and THEN added
        // the carrier's velocity, overshooting by one frame's travel -- over 1.5 u (the old snap)
        // from about 90 u/s -- so it jittered beside the slot forever
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.W, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.W, Pressed = true });
        float yaw0 = ship.Rotation; bool allHome = false; float topSeen = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 15 && !allHome; i++)
        {
            allHome = Bombers().All(w => w.Docked); topSeen = Mathf.Max(topSeen, ship.Velocity.Length());
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.W, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.W, Pressed = false });
        Check(allHome && topSeen > 90f && Bombers().All(w => w.RearmLeft > 0 || w.Armed),
              $"with the carrier at full speed ({topSeen:0} u/s), the bombers still dock and re-arm");
        ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(15.0);
        // the balance's reference: the carrier's MEASURED output (19.07 DPS over 90 s when set); re-measured
        // here over 40 s -- lumpier (bomber strikes land in lumps), so within 25% -- to catch it drifting
        {
            var md = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Click(Scr(md.Position)); await Wait(0.1);
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); md.ResetMeter();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05); Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 40; i++)
            {
                ship.Position = md.Position + new Vector2(0, 520); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                if (i % 30 == 0 && ship.BombersReady == (int)ship.Stats["bomber_count"] && ship.StrikeTarget == null) Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            double carrierDps = md.Total / 40.0;
            // (the reference was measured unequipped; the five default chips add 25%: 19.07 x 1.25 = 23.84)
            GD.Print($"  carrier measured: {carrierDps:0.00} DPS over 40 s (reference 23.84 with the chips)");
            Check(Math.Abs(carrierDps / 23.84 - 1) < 0.25, $"the carrier still delivers about the measured 19.07 DPS (x1.25 with the chips = 23.84) the balance rests on ({carrierDps:0.00})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(12.0);                       // recall the wing for what follows
        }

        // No tracking, tested on single torpedoes so bombers cannot re-aim: moving
        // the target during a whole strike is not a fair test, because a bomber still
        // lining up correctly aims at where the target now is.
        var home = d1.Position;
        var from = home + new Vector2(0, 500);
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5); d1.ResetMeter();
        Combat.LaunchTorpedo(from, Vector2.Up, 240f, 900f, 3.0);
        await Wait(3.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 3) < 0.01, "control: a torpedo at a still target hits for 3");
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5); d1.ResetMeter();
        Combat.LaunchTorpedo(from, Vector2.Up, 240f, 900f, 3.0);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        d1.Position = home + new Vector2(160, 0);                  // step aside just after launch
        await Wait(4.5);
        GD.Print($"  moved target took {d1.Total:0} damage");
        Check(d1.Total == 0, "a target that moves after launch is missed (the torpedo does not track)");
        d1.Position = home;

        // ── utility ships: hull, loss, and a rebuild for 10% of the category's investment ──
        {
            var Yd = H.Yard;
            var mn = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
            var sv = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager);
            Check(mn.MaxHull == 120 && sv.MaxHull == 120 && Yd.Hauler.MaxHull == 150, "miners and salvagers have 120 hull, the hauler 150");
            Yd.Credits = 100000; double inv0 = Yd.Invested("MINERS"); int lv0 = Yd.Level("miner_hold");
            double price = Economy.Cost(Economy.ById("miner_hold"), lv0);
            Check(Yd.TryBuy("miner_hold") && Math.Abs(Yd.Invested("MINERS") - inv0 - price) < 1e-6, $"buying an upgrade adds its price to its category's investment (+{price:0})");
            double cost = Yd.RebuildCost("MINERS");
            Check(cost > 0 && Math.Abs(cost - Math.Round(Yd.Invested("MINERS") * 0.10)) < 1e-6, $"a rebuild costs 10% of it ({cost:0} of {Yd.Invested("MINERS"):0})");
            mn.TakeDamage(119);
            Check(mn.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed && Math.Abs(mn.Hull - 1) < 1e-6, "119 damage leaves a miner at 1 hull");
            mn.TakeDamage(5); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(mn.State == Gatherer.St.Destroyed && !mn.Visible && mn.Cargo == 0 && Math.Abs(mn.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2 && Yd.ArmOf(mn) < 0 && Yd.QueueIndex(mn) < 0,
                  "destroyed: gone, its cargo lost, its arm and queue place given up, rebuilt in 30 s");
            Yd.Credits = cost - 1; mn.RebuildIn = 0.1; await Wait(0.3);
            Check(mn.State == Gatherer.St.Destroyed && mn.WaitingForCredits, "short of the rebuild cost, it waits");
            Yd.Credits = cost + 500; await Wait(0.2);
            Check(mn.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed && mn.Hull == 120 && mn.Visible && Math.Abs(Yd.Credits - 500) < 1e-6, "paid for: rebuilt at the base at full hull, exactly 10% charged");
            // the hull upgrades: +10% a level, 125 cr to start (25% above the other +10% rows), x1.25 a level
            var mh = Economy.ById("miner_hull"); var sh2 = Economy.ById("salvager_hull");
            Check(mh != null && sh2 != null && mh.Tab == "MINERS" && sh2.Tab == "SALVAGERS"
                  && Economy.Cost(mh, 0) == 125 && Economy.Cost(mh, 1) == 156 && Economy.Cost(sh2, 0) == 125,
                  "Miner hull and Salvager hull: 125 cr to start, x1.25 a level (156 next)");
            var fullM = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner && g.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed);
            fullM.Hull = fullM.MaxHull; Yd.Credits = 100000;
            Yd.TryBuy("miner_hull"); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(fullM.MaxHull == 132 && fullM.Hull == 132 && sv.MaxHull == 120, "one level: a miner's hull 132 (+10%, kept full); salvagers still 120");
            Yd.TryBuy("salvager_hull"); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(sv.MaxHull == 132, "and the salvagers' own upgrade: 132");
            double hcost = Yd.RebuildCost("HAULER");
            Yd.Hauler.ResetToPad(); Yd.Hauler.TakeDamage(150); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Destroyed && Yd.Hauler.WarpedOut && Math.Abs(Yd.Hauler.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2, "the hauler, destroyed: gone for 30 s");
            Yd.Hauler.RebuildIn = 0.1; Yd.Credits = hcost + 100; await Wait(0.3);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Yd.Hauler.Hull == 150 && Math.Abs(Yd.Credits - 100) < 1e-6,
                  $"rebuilt on its pad for 10% of the hauler's investment ({hcost:0} cr)");
        }

        // ── networking: silent offline; what friends elsewhere need; one press a second ──
        {
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle, "single player: no socket, no web lookup, no router job running");
            var inet = Net.Describe("203.0.113.7", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var shared = Net.Describe("100.72.1.9", true, "", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var dbl = Net.Describe("198.51.100.4", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var manual = Net.Describe("", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var none = Net.Describe("", false, "", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            Check(inet.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && inet.address == "203.0.113.7:27015", "router opened the port and is the internet's edge: friends join public:port");
            Check(shared.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly && dbl.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly,
                  "a private router address, or one that differs from the public address: another NAT is in the way, network only");
            Check(manual.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && manual.address == "203.0.113.7:27015" && manual.message.Contains("forward UDP 27015"),
                  "the router will not open it: the public address, and how to forward the port");
            Check(none.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly, "nothing learned: network only");
            Check(new[] { inet, shared, dbl, manual, none }.All(d => !d.message.Contains("203.0.113.7")), "no status line ever prints the public address");
            var sm = Nodes<SessionMenu>().First();
            var off = Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "Offline");
            off.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(sm.Locked && new[] { "Host", "Join", "Offline" }.All(n => Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == n).Disabled),
                  "one press a second: after a press, host, join and offline wait");
            await Wait(SessionMenu.PressGap);
            Check(!sm.Locked && !Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "Host").Disabled, "and are back after 1 s");
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle, "and offline stays silent");
            // review: a router answer for a session that is already over must never be kept --
            // its port mapping is closed at once (it used to be stored, leaking the forward)
            var nf = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
            int genNow = (int)typeof(Net).GetField("_hostGen", nf).GetValue(Net.I);
            int closed0 = Net.I.StaleMappingsClosed;
            typeof(Net).GetMethod("RouterResult", nf).Invoke(Net.I, new object[] { genNow - 1, 27015, "203.0.113.9", "", new Upnp() });
            Check(typeof(Net).GetField("_upnp", nf).GetValue(Net.I) == null && Net.I.StaleMappingsClosed == closed0 + 1,
                  "a router answer for a finished session is not kept, and its port mapping is closed");
            Check(Net.IsTailnet("100.100.1.2") && !Net.IsTailnet("100.63.0.1") && !Net.IsTailnet("192.168.1.2"),
                  "Tailscale addresses (100.64.0.0/10) are recognised for friends on a tailnet");
        }

        // ── equipment (I): per-class gear; common defaults and five chips (+5% damage, +5% hull each) ──
        {
            Check(Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Carrier)[0] == "cv_fighter_hangars" && Equipment.ById("cv_fighter_hangars").Name == "Mk I Fighter Hangars"
                  && Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Battleship).Take(5).All(id => Equipment.ById(id).Rarity == Rarity.Common),
                  "the defaults: common parts in five slots; a carrier's weapon slot holds its Fighter Hangars");
            var bsE = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship, Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Battleship, Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Battleship)));
            var cvE = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier, Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Carrier, Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Carrier)));
            Check(Math.Abs(bsE["hull"] - 375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bsE["main_damage"] - 7.375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(cvE["hull"] - 250) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(cvE["fighter_damage"] - 2.5) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(cvE["torpedo_damage"] - 18.75) < 1e-6,
                  "five default chips: +25% damage and hull (battleship 375 hull, 7.375 a shell; carrier 250 hull, fighters 2.5, torpedoes 18.75)");
            var cls0 = Me.Class;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            var ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(ew != null && Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Slot_")) == 5
                  && Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Chip_")) == 5, "I opens the equipment: five parts and five chips");
            double full = Me.MaxHp;
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Unequip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full * 1.20 / 1.25) < 1e-6 && Character.SparesFor(cls0).Count == 1, $"a chip off: 5% less hull ({full:0} -> {Me.MaxHp:0}), the chip kept as a spare");
            var other = cls0 == ShipClass.Battleship ? ShipClass.Carrier : ShipClass.Battleship;
            Me.SetClass(other); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(other).Skip(5).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 5, "the other class keeps its own gear: five chips");
            Me.SetClass(cls0); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).Skip(5).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 4 && Character.SparesFor(cls0).Count == 1 && Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full * 1.20 / 1.25) < 1e-6,
                  "back on the first class: its gear as it was left (four chips, one spare)");
            ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            if (ew == null) { Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2); ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().First(); }
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Equip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full) < 1e-6 && Character.SparesFor(cls0).Count == 0, "the spare back on: full hull again");
            Character.Save(); var saved = (string[])Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).Clone();
            string eqId = Character.Id; Character.Loadout.Clear(); Character.Load(eqId);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).SequenceEqual(saved), "the gear is saved with the pilot");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().Count() == 0, "Esc closes it");
        }

        // ── pilot progression: EXP, levels, points, flat upgrades ──
        {
            Check(Progression.Cost(0) == 1 && Progression.Cost(1) == 2 && Progression.Cost(2) == 3, "upgrade costs 1, 2, 3, ... points");
            Check(Progression.ExpToNext == 1000 && Progression.ExpToNext == 1000, "every level needs 1000 EXP");
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought);
            int up = Progression.AddExp(2600);
            Check(up == 2 && Character.Level == 3 && Character.Exp == 600 && Character.Points == 2, "2600 EXP from level 1: level 3, 600 over, 2 points");
            // the approved formula, in literal numbers (so the checks can fail)
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.S(1) - 1) < 1e-12 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(5) - 1.4641) < 1e-9, "boss levels start at 1: S(L) = 1.1^(L-1) (level 5: x1.4641)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.HullMult(1, 3) - 2.2) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.DamageMult(1, 3) - 1.4) < 1e-9, "a party of 3: boss hull x2.2, damage x1.4");
            Check(Missions.KillExpFor(5, 10) == 100 && Missions.KillExpFor(2, 1) == 400 && Missions.KillExpFor(1, 1) == 200, "kill EXP 200 x boss level / pilot level (5 vs 10: 100; 2 vs 1: 400)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(1, 1) - 2000) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(1, 2) - 1500) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(1, 3) - 4000.0 / 3) < 1e-9,
                  "the bounty, +50% per extra pilot, split: 2000 solo, 1500 each for two, 1333 each for three");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
            int k1 = Progression.AwardBossKill(1), k2 = Progression.AwardBossKill(1);
            Check(k1 == 550 && k2 == 300, $"a level-1 boss: 200 + 250 first clear + 100 = 550; again: 300 ({k1}, {k2})");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 3;
            Check(!Nodes<BossBar>().First().Visible, "outside the arena there is no boss bar");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            var pw = Nodes<PilotWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(pw != null, "L opens the PILOT window");
            Button PBuy(string id) => Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(pw).First(r => r.Name == "Pilot_" + id)).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
            var me = Me; double hull0 = me.MaxHp, hp0 = me.Hp;
            Character.Points = 3;
            PBuy("hull").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            // (points are added BEFORE percentage bonuses: the five default chips' +25% makes +5 into +6.25)
            Check(Character.Bought[1] == 1 && Character.Points == 2 && Math.Abs(me.MaxHp - (hull0 + 6.25)) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(me.Hp - (hp0 + 6.25)) < 1e-6,
                  "Hull (1 pt): +5 hull (x1.25 by the default chips: +6.25) on the ship at once");
            PBuy("hull").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Character.Bought[1] == 2 && Character.Points == 0 && Math.Abs(me.MaxHp - (hull0 + 12.5)) < 1e-6, "Hull again (2 pts): +10 in all (+12.5 with the chips)");
            Check(PBuy("speed").Disabled, "with no points left, BUY is disabled");
            Character.Points = 6;
            PBuy("damage").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); PBuy("speed").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); PBuy("turn").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            var baseSheet = new ShipStats(me.Class, Equipment.Bonuses(me.Class, me.Loadout));            // the same gear, no points
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Stats[Progression.DamageStat(me.Class)] - baseSheet[Progression.DamageStat(me.Class)] - 1.25) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(me.Stats["max_speed"] - baseSheet["max_speed"] - 1) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(me.Stats["turn_rate"] - baseSheet["turn_rate"] - Mathf.DegToRad(1f)) < 1e-6,
                  "Weapons +1 damage (x1.25 by the chips) on the main weapon, Engines +1 speed, Rudder +1 degree/s");
            string cid = Character.Id; int[] saved = (int[])Character.Bought.Clone();
            Character.Save(); System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought); Character.Load(cid);
            Check(Character.Bought.SequenceEqual(saved) && Character.Level == 3, "purchases and level are saved with the character");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<PilotWindow>() == 0, "L closes it");
            // leave the pilot as found for what follows
            System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought); Character.Points = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.Exp = 0; Character.Save();
            Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity");
        }

        // ── damage rules: one hit per source per 0.35 s; regeneration; 12 s out of combat ──
        {
            var me = Me; double hp0 = me.Hp;
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a"); me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a");
            Check(Math.Abs(hp0 - me.Hp - 5) < 0.2, "the same source lands only once inside 0.35 s");
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:b");
            Check(Math.Abs(hp0 - me.Hp - 10) < 0.3, "a different source lands at once");
            await Wait(0.55); double hp1 = me.Hp;
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a");
            Check(hp1 - me.Hp > 4.5, "after 0.52 s the first source lands again");
            Check(PlayerShip.CombatHold == 12, "out of combat means 12 s without dealing or taking damage");
            me.TakeDamage(me.Hp - me.MaxHp * 0.5); me.NoteCombat(); double h0 = me.Hp;
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp - 0.005) < 0.0015, $"in combat it regenerates 0.5% of max hull a second ({(me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_combatT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0);
            double h1 = me.Hp; await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp - 0.03) < 0.004, $"out of combat, 3% a second ({(me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6a): the light fighter -- cruise, boost, post, pin, laser ──
        {
            var me = Me; var keepPos = me.Position;
            me.Position = new Vector2(4000, 2500); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var r1 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(0, 2300));
            await Wait(0.5); var rp0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float cruise = (r1.Position - rp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r1.Target == me && Math.Abs(cruise - 100f) < 8f && !r1.Boosting, $"a light raider goes for the nearest ship at an unupgraded capital ship's pace ({cruise:0} u/s)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Raider.BoostAt - 1600f) < 1e-3, "it boosts from 1600 u (3 s at 500%, plus its 100 u reach)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !r1.Boosting; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float atBoost = r1.Position.DistanceTo(me.Position); var b0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.4);
            float rboost = (r1.Position - b0).Length() / 0.4f;
            Check(Math.Abs(atBoost - 1600) < 30 && Math.Abs(rboost - 500) < 30, $"at 1600 u ({atBoost:0}) it boosts to 500% ({rboost:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !r1.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float rheld = Raider.Gap(r1.Position, me);                 // from the HULL: beside the ship, never on it
            Check(r1.Latched && rheld > 60 && rheld <= Raider.PinRange, $"it lands near its target, not on top: posted {rheld:0} u off the hull");
            var r2 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(300, 0)); var r3 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(-300, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !(r2.Latched && r3.Latched); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var bow = Vector2.Up.Rotated(me.Rotation);
            var angs = new[] { r1, r2, r3 }.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bow.AngleTo(r.Position - me.Position))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
            Check(r2.Latched && r3.Latched && Math.Abs(angs[0] + 90) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[1]) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[2] - 90) < 20,
                  $"three hold posts ahead, left and right ({string.Join(", ", angs.Select(a => a.ToString("0")))} deg off the bow)");
            Check(me.Pinned, "rheld there, they pin it");
            float rot0 = me.Rotation;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = true });
            await Wait(1.0);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = false });
            float rspd = me.Velocity.Length(), rtop = (float)me.Stats["max_speed"];
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(rot0, me.Rotation)) < 0.01f && rspd <= rtop * 0.2f + 1f && me.SpeedAhead > 5f,
                  $"pinned: A does not turn it, and forced thrust holds it to 20% of top speed ({rspd:0} of {rtop:0} u/s)");
            double Raided() => me.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key.StartsWith("raider:")).Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double dm0 = Raided(); await Wait(5.0);
            double dps = (Raided() - dm0) / 5.0;
            Check(Math.Abs(dps - 3.0) < 0.7, $"three lights, 1 DPS each ({dps:0.00} DPS)");
            foreach (var r in new[] { r1, r2, r3 }) r.TakeDamage(999);
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0 && !Combat.Hostiles.OfType<Raider>().Any() && !me.Pinned, "shot down, they are gone -- and the pin lets go");
            var mnr = H.Yard.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner && g.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed);
            var r4 = H.SpawnRaider(mnr.Position + new Vector2(120, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !r4.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float mr0 = mnr.Rotation; var mp0 = mnr.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float rmspd = (mnr.Position - mp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r4.Target == mnr && mnr.Pinned && Math.Abs(rmspd - (float)mnr.Speed * 0.2f) < 3f && Math.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(mr0, mnr.Rotation)) < 0.01f,
                  $"a miner is pinned the same way: forward at 20%, no turning ({rmspd:0} u/s)");
            r4.TakeDamage(999); await Wait(0.3);
            me.Position = keepPos; me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));   // hand over at full hull
        }

        // ── raiders (6b): the heavy -- waits at the map's edge; a predicted missile; boosts at 700% on a pinned target ──
        {
            var hm = Me; var keepH = hm.Position;
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2000, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var hv = H.SpawnRaider(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 500), RaiderKind.Heavy);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hv.Heavy && Math.Abs(hv.Length - 4 * Raider.LightLength) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(Raider.HeavyDps - 2 * Raider.RaiderDps) < 1e-9 && Raider.RaiderDps == 1,
                  "a heavy raider: four times a light's length, twice the raider damage (2 DPS)");
            double HvLaser() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"raider:{hv.NetId}").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double HvMissile() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"heavy:{hv.NetId}:missile").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            var edge = Raider.EdgeSpot(hm.Position);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) > 8f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.5);
            float hvFacing = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hv.Rotation, (hm.Position - hv.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f)));
            Check(hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) < 10f && Math.Abs(hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - 3200f) < 10f && hvFacing < 15f && HvLaser() == 0,
                  $"its target unpinned, it waits at the map's edge nearest it ({hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} u from the base), facing it, laser silent");
            // the missile: within 500 u of the heavy, at where the target will be in 7 s
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 420f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var blastRing = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 1 && blastRing != null && blastRing.A.DistanceTo(hm.Position) < 6f && Math.Abs(blastRing.Duration - 7) < 1e-6,
                  "within 500 u it fires at where the target will be; a red circle marks it for 7 s");
            double mm0 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 9 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(HvMissile() - mm0 - Raider.MissileDamage) < 1e-6, $"staying on the circle, the blast lands ({HvMissile() - mm0:0})");
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 300); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;                         // off the circle
            double mm1 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 9 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 0 && HvMissile() == mm1, "moving off the circle dodges it");
            // anything out of place -- a wild speed, a broken number -- and it aims right at the target
            var pz = new Vector2(100, 200);
            Check(Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(10, 0)) == pz + new Vector2(70, 0) && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(5000, 0)) == pz
                  && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(float.NaN, 0)) == pz && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(float.PositiveInfinity, 1)) == pz,
                  "the missile leads a sane speed 7 s; a wild speed, NaN or infinity: it aims at where the target is");
            // live: 2700 u/s in 45 u steps (wild, yet under the 50 u jump filter) -- the circle must sit on the ship
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 400f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.12);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            int rings0 = Nodes<Telegraph>().Count(t => !t.Line);
            Vector2 atFire = hm.Position;
            for (int i = 0; i < 20 && Nodes<Telegraph>().Count(t => !t.Line) == rings0; i++)
            {
                atFire = hm.Position; hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 45);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var wildRing = Nodes<Telegraph>().LastOrDefault(t => !t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(wildRing != null && wildRing.A.DistanceTo(atFire) < 100f,
                  $"a target moving at a wild 2700 u/s: the circle sits on it, not 7 s ahead ({(wildRing == null ? -1 : wildRing.A.DistanceTo(atFire)):0} u off)");
            // pinned: it boosts at 700% until 300 u away, then closes at cruise and fires 2 DPS
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(1900, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f;
            var lights = new[] { H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(0, -250)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(250, 0)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(-250, 0)) };
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hm.Pinned; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.1); var hp1 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float burn = (hv.Position - hp1).Length() / 0.2f;
            // the spec's numbers as literals: a check that reads the code's own constant cannot catch it being wrong
            Check(hm.Pinned && Math.Abs(burn - 700f) < 60f, $"its target pinned, it boosts at 700% ({burn:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(hm.Position) > 300f - 20f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var hp2 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float slow = (hv.Position - hp2).Length() / 0.2f;
            // (over the ground: cruise plus the pinned target's own forced thrust, ~20 u/s)
            Check(slow <= 100f + 0.2f * (float)hm.Stats["max_speed"] + 5f, $"inside 300 u it closes at cruise ({slow:0} u/s over the ground)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hv.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double hl0 = HvLaser(); await Wait(3.0);
            double hdps = (HvLaser() - hl0) / 3.0;
            Check(hv.Latched && Raider.Gap(hv.Position, hm) <= Raider.HeavyReach && Math.Abs(hdps - 2) < 0.4,
                  $"from 135 u off the hull its laser does 2 DPS ({hdps:0.00})");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            hm.Position = keepH; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.TakeDamage(-(hm.MaxHp - hm.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6c): patrols -- 3 lights and 1 heavy; nothing within 2000 u, no target ──
        {
            int pid = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 6500));
            var pat = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == pid).ToList();
            Check(pat.Count == 4 && pat.Count(r => !r.Heavy) == 3 && pat.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1, "a patrol: three lights and one heavy");
            await Wait(1.0);
            float pd0 = pat[0].Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos);
            Check(pat.All(r => r.Target == null) && pd0 < 6500 - 60 && pd0 > 6500 - 200,
                  $"nothing within 2000 u: they take no target, and head for the perimeter at a patrol's pace ({6500 - pd0:0} u in 1 s)");
            var pm = Me; var keepP = pm.Position;
            pm.Position = pat[0].Position + new Vector2(0, -1500); pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.5);
            var heavyP = pat.First(r => r.Heavy);
            Check(pat.Where(r => !r.Heavy).All(r => r.Target == pm) && heavyP.Target == pm,
                  "a ship within 2000 u: the lights go for it, and their heavy takes the same target");
            foreach (var r in pat) if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(r)) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            pm.Position = keepP; pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; pm.TakeDamage(-(pm.MaxHp - pm.Hp));
        }

        // ── the base's own weapons: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u, a tracking 25-damage missile at 600 u -- raiders only ──
        {
            var bd = Nodes<BaseDefense>().First();
            Check(BaseDefense.LaserRange == 300 && BaseDefense.MissileRange == 600 && BaseDefense.LaserDps == 5 && BaseDefense.MissileDamage == 25
                  && Math.Abs(BaseDefense.MissileSpeed - 1.2f * 100f) < 1e-3, "the base: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u; a 25-damage missile at 600 u, 120 u/s");
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"); var dHome = dd.Position;
            dd.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 250); dd.ResetMeter();
            double l0 = bd.LaserDealt; int m0 = bd.MissilesFired; await Wait(2.0);
            Check(bd.LaserDealt == l0 && bd.MissilesFired == m0 && dd.Total == 0, "it never fires at the practice dummies");
            dd.Position = dHome;
            var spot1 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 280);
            var rb = H.SpawnRaider(spot1); rb.Hp = 1000;
            double lz = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 120; i++) { rb.Position = spot1; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            double laser2 = bd.LaserDealt - lz;
            Check(Math.Abs(laser2 - 10) < 2.6, $"a raider inside 300 u: the laser burns it at 5 DPS ({laser2:0.0} in 2 s)");
            rb.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot2 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 500);
            var rc = H.SpawnRaider(spot2); rc.Hp = 1000;
            int mz = bd.MissilesFired; double lz2 = bd.LaserDealt;
            Torpedo bm = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && bm == null; i++)
            {
                rc.Position = spot2;
                bm = Nodes<Torpedo>().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.HostileFire && !t.Heavy && t.TargetId == rc.NetId);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var bp1 = bm?.GlobalPosition ?? Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            float bspd = bm != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(bm) ? (bm.GlobalPosition - bp1).Length() / 0.25f : 0f;
            double hpc = rc.Hp;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rc.Hp == hpc; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bm != null && bd.MissilesFired > mz && bd.LaserDealt == lz2 && Math.Abs(bspd - 120f) < 6f && Math.Abs(hpc - rc.Hp - 25) < 1e-6,
                  $"a raider at 500 u: no laser, but a tracking missile at {bspd:0} u/s lands for {hpc - rc.Hp:0}");
            rc.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot3 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 700);
            var rd = H.SpawnRaider(spot3); rd.Hp = 1000;
            int mw = bd.MissilesFired; double lw = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6; i++) { rd.Position = spot3; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bd.MissilesFired == mw && bd.LaserDealt == lw, "a raider at 700 u: out of reach, nothing fires");
            rd.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
        }

        // ── enemy fire, shields, stasis, the escape pod, re-boarding ──
        {
            var d3x = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
            Check(d3x.Armed && !H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1").Armed, "only the bottom-right dummy is armed");
            var sh = Me;
            double Dummy() => sh.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("?", out var v) ? v : 0;    // (hull regenerates: count the hit itself)
            double hit0 = Dummy();
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -120); sh.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; sh.Rotation = 0f;
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && Dummy() == hit0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(Dummy() - hit0 - 50) < 0.01, $"the armed dummy's missile hits a ship within 150 u for 50 ({Dummy() - hit0:0})");
            var shield = Nodes<ShieldFlash>(sh).First();
            Check(shield.Glow(2) > 0 && shield.Glow(0) == 0, "the shield lights the side facing the dummy (astern), not the bow");
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -1200); await Wait(6.0);
            double taken1 = sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum(); await Wait(6.0);        // (hull regenerates: count damage instead)
            Check(sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum() == taken1, "outside 150 u it holds fire");
            Check(sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (PlayerShip.Art[sh.Class].Length * 0.45f), 0f)
                  && !sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (PlayerShip.Art[sh.Class].HalfWidth + 12f), 0f),
                  "the collider is a capsule: the bow is inside, a point just off the beam is not");
            // death
            sh.TakeDamage(sh.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            var pod = Nodes<EscapePod>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(!sh.Alive && sh.StasisLeft > 119 && pod != null && !Combat.Hostiles.Contains(pod as IHittable) && !Combat.Players.Any(p => (object)p == pod),
                  "destroyed: stasis (2:00) and an escape pod that nothing can target");
            Check(pod != null && Math.Abs(pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Texture.GetHeight() * pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Scale.Y - 24f) < 0.5f,
                  "the pod is 24 u long (60% of a salvager's 40)");
            var podStart = pod.Position;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W);
            Check(pod.Position.DistanceTo(podStart) > 20f && sh.Velocity == Vector2.Zero, "W flies the pod; the ship stays put in stasis");
            Check(H.GetType().GetField("_cam", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance) != null && sh.ViewPosition == pod.Position, "the camera follows the pod");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!sh.Alive, "F does nothing before stasis ends");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_stasis", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(sh, 0.5);
            await Wait(0.7);
            Check(sh.CanReboard, "after the timer the ship is ready");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(sh.Alive && sh.Hp >= sh.MaxHp * 0.33 - 1e-6 && sh.Hp < sh.MaxHp * 0.34 && Nodes<EscapePod>().Count() == 0, "F re-boards at 33% hull (regenerating from there); the pod is gone");
            sh.TakeDamage(-(sh.MaxHp - sh.Hp));  // (test only) back to full for what follows
        }

        // a double-click on empty space clears the target; a single click there does not
        {
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            var empty = Me.Position + new Vector2(-260, -260);
            Click(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a single click on empty space keeps the target");
            DoubleClick(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == null, "a double-click on empty space clears it");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            DoubleClick(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a double-click on a target keeps it selected");
        }

        // selection rules still hold
        await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300));
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Tab); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d1, "Tab selects the enemy nearest the ship");
        Click(Scr(d2.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d2, "left-click selects another");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == null && H != null, "Esc clears the target before leaving");

        // placement hook
        Vector2? placedAt = null;
        H.BeginPlacement("TEST", p => placedAt = p);
        var spot = d1.Position + new Vector2(-150, -150);
        Click(Scr(spot)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(placedAt.HasValue && placedAt.Value.DistanceTo(spot) < 3f && !H.Placing, "left-click confirms a placement at the cursor");

        // wings: class swap and rebuild leave no orphans
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == 0, "to battleship: wings gone");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier);
        Priv(H, "RebuildShips"); await Wait(0.1);
        int craft = (int)(Me.Stats["fighter_count"] + Me.Stats["bomber_count"]);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == craft && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, $"rebuild leaves no orphan wings ({Count<Wing>()} of {craft})");

        // ── camera: wheel zoom, the free camera (Y), its tether ──
        {
            var sh = Me;
            void Wheel(MouseButton b) { Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = true, Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) }); }
            for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelDown); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom / Hub.ZoomOutMax), $"wheel out stops at 33% further out ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelUp); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom * Hub.ZoomInMax), $"wheel in stops at 1.5x ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom * 0.8f); float kept = H.ZoomLevel;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.FreeCamera, "Y frees the camera");
            var cam0 = H.CameraPosition;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X > 800, $"arrow keys move it ({H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X:0} u in 1 s)");
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(6.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            float tether = H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(tether - sh.MyArt.CameraRange) < 1f && sh.MyArt.CameraRange == 5000f, $"it stops 5000 u from the ship ({tether:0})");
            var cam1 = H.CameraPosition;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)) });
            await Wait(0.5);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X < cam1.X - 100f, "the mouse against the screen edge pans it too");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(!H.FreeCamera && H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition) < 30f && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, kept),
                  "Y again returns to the ship, keeping the zoom");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom);
        }

        // ── buildings open menus; READY -> autopilot -> portal -> ARENA -> home ──
        // (Late in the run on purpose: the trip replaces the whole hub scene, so every
        //  node reference taken before it -- dummies, ships -- is stale afterwards.)
        {
            var tioHome = Me.Position;
            Me.Position = Hub.TioPos + new Vector2(260, 0); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.TioPos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.TioOpen && Nodes<TioWindow>().Any(), "left-click on the TIO opens WARP TO TARGET");
            var tw = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(!Nodes<Button>(tw).Any(b => b.Name == "Warp"), "no WARP button: READY drives the mission");
            H.Yard.Credits = 1000;
            // start well away from where the portal opens, so the autopilot has a real trip
            Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos + new Vector2(300, 700); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
            Nodes<Button>(tw).First(b => b.Name == "Ready").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.AllReady && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Opening && Me.AutopilotTo == H.MissionPortalPos,
                  "READY: the party (just me) is ready, the portal starts opening, and my ship flies itself there");
            await Wait(Hub.PortalOpenTime + 0.2);
            var mp = H.GetNodeOrNull<Node2D>("MissionPortal");
            var tio = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("TIO"); float halfW = tio.GetRect().Size.X * tio.Scale.X / 2f;
            Check(H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen && mp != null && mp.Position.X > Hub.TioPos.X + halfW && mp.Position.Y < Hub.TioPos.Y - Hub.TioHeight / 2f,
                  "after 3 s the portal opens off the TIO's top-right corner");
            float d0 = Me.Position.DistanceTo(H.MissionPortalPos);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 45 && !Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"the capital-ship autopilot flew {d0:0} u to the portal and the party went through");
            var boss = H.Boss;
            Check(boss != null && boss.Alive && H.Yard == null && !Nodes<TargetDummy>().Any() && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(boss),
                  "the arena: the Silver Lancer, and no base or dummies");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            var bb = Nodes<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "BaseButton");
            Check(bb != null && !bb.IsVisibleInTree(), "in the arena the BASE button is hidden");
            bb?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);                 // and even pressed, there is nothing to open
            Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "neither B nor the button opens a base menu out there");
            Check(Music.CombatZone, "the arena counts as combat for the music");
            Me.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.Rotation = 0;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(0.1);                           // point defence up
            Check(Math.Abs(boss.MaxHp - 600) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(Boss.GunDamage / Boss.GunEvery - 3) < 1e-9, "the boss: 600 hull at level 1 (solo); its guns 3 DPS");
            // stand in the beam on purpose with a thick hull (test only), and watch the rhythm
            // the boss's health bar: a chunk per hit, bouncing, whitening, fading; bursts merge
            var bar = Nodes<BossBar>().First();
            Check(bar.Visible, "in the arena the boss's health bar shows");
            double hpA = boss.Hp;
            boss.TakeDamage(60); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var ck = bar.Chunks.LastOrDefault();
            Check(ck != null && Math.Abs(ck.To - hpA / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ck.From - (hpA - 60) / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6,
                  "a hit leaves a chunk: exactly the slice of the bar it took");
            await Wait(0.06);
            float off = BossBar.Offset(ck.T), wh = BossBar.Whiteness(ck.T);
            Check(Math.Abs(off) > 1f && wh > 0f && wh < 1f, $"the chunk bobs ({off:0.0} px) and is turning white ({wh:0.00})");
            Check(BossBar.Offset(BossBar.BounceFor) == 0 && BossBar.Alpha(BossBar.Life) <= 0.001 && BossBar.Whiteness(BossBar.Life * 0.5) >= 0.999,
                  "the bounce dies away; white by half-life; gone by the end");
            await Wait(BossBar.Life + 0.1);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 0, "and then it is gone from the bar");
            // one small hit a frame for three frames (50 ms): inside the 60 ms window, so one chunk
            // (the bar reads the hull once a frame, so hits within ONE frame could never test this)
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { boss.TakeDamage(1); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 1, $"a burst of small hits, frame after frame, shares one chunk ({bar.Chunks.Count})");
            var me2 = Me; me2.MaxHp = 5000; me2.TakeDamage(-(5000 - me2.Hp));
            double tBeam = -1, tCharge = -1, tVolley = -1, arenaT = 0; int trident = 0; float spread = 0;
            async void Watch()
            {
                while (Hub.InArena && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(boss))
                {
                    arenaT += 1.0 / 60;
                    // The beam's place in the rhythm is where it COMMITS -- its escorts going out.
                    // Its red line comes later by the escorts' predicted web plus a second, so
                    // timing the rhythm off the telegraph would measure that delay, not the rhythm.
                    if (tBeam < 0 && H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort)) tBeam = arenaT;
                    foreach (var tl in Nodes<Telegraph>().Where(x => x.Line))
                        if (tCharge < 0 && Math.Abs(tl.Width - Boss.HalfWidth * 2f) < 0.1f) tCharge = arenaT;
                    if (tVolley < 0 && boss.Volleys > 0)
                    {
                        tVolley = arenaT;
                        var big = Nodes<Torpedo>().Where(x => x.HostileFire && x.Size == 2f).ToList();
                        trident = big.Count;
                        if (big.Count == 3) spread = big.Max(x => x.Dir.Angle()) - big.Min(x => x.Dir.Angle());
                    }
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                }
            }
            Watch();
            // The death beam OPENS WITH ITS ESCORTS, well before any red line: two fragile lights
            // 45 degrees to port and starboard of the nose, shivering on the spot while they come
            // round onto the pilot. The beam only starts charging once their web should have landed.
            var escorts = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Raider>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && escorts.Count < 2; i++) { escorts = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            var bnose = Vector2.Up.Rotated(boss.Rotation);
            var escAng = escorts.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bnose.AngleTo(r.Position - boss.Position))).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
            Check(escorts.Count == 2 && Math.Abs(escAng[0] + 45) < 12 && Math.Abs(escAng[1] - 45) < 12
                  && escorts.All(r => r.Target == me2 && r.Hp > 0 && r.Hp <= Boss.EscortHull * Missions.S(Missions.Level) + 1e-6),
                  $"two escorts launch at the pilot, 45 degrees to port and starboard ({string.Join(", ", escAng.Select(x => x.ToString("0")))}), fragile");
            Check(escorts.All(r => r.Shivering), "the escorts shiver at the launch point before the run in");
            Check(Nodes<Telegraph>().All(t => !t.Line), "no beam line yet: the escorts go first, the charge follows their web");
            // the bar under the boss's hull resets as a super commits and counts the 15 s to the next
            Check(boss.SuperFill < 0.15 && Math.Abs(boss.NextSuperIn - 15) < 1.5,
                  $"the super-move bar resets as the beam commits, counting the 15 s to the ram ({boss.NextSuperIn:0.0} s, fill {boss.SuperFill:0.00})");
            Telegraph beam = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && beam == null; i++) { beam = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Line); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(beam != null && boss.TelegraphsPending > 0, "the death beam is telegraphed: a red line before it fires");
            Check(Math.Abs(beam.Duration - Boss.BeamWindup) < 1e-6 && Boss.BeamWindup == 6, "the beam charges 6 s");
            // the warning is parented to the boss, so it swings with the hull instead of being
            // pinned to the spot the boss stood on: the line drawn IS the line fired
            Check(beam.GetParent() == boss, "the beam's telegraph rides the hull rather than the world");
            // Push it out past its 650 u standoff BEFORE measuring. Inside that range it would
            // not close anyway, so watching for drift there proves nothing -- a mutant that
            // ignored the lock completely still passed this. Out here it wants to close at
            // 30 u/s, which is 22 u over the window below, so holding still is a real claim.
            var bossWasAt = boss.Position;
            boss.Position += (boss.Position - me2.Position).Normalized() * 700f;
            var lockAt = boss.Position;
            double standoff = boss.Position.DistanceTo(me2.Position);
            await Wait(0.75);
            Check(boss.BeamCharging && boss.Locked && standoff > 650 && boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt) < 0.01f,
                  $"the boss is locked in place while the beam charges, {standoff:0} u out where it would otherwise close (drifted {boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt):0.000} u)");
            boss.Position = bossWasAt;   // put it back: left out there its tridents take so long to
                                         // arrive that the point-defence check below times out
            // Reach, not trigger: activation is still the 30 s rhythm, the nearest pilot and the
            // escorts' predicted web -- only how far the thing carries has changed. Compared with
            // the spec's number as a literal, never with BeamLength itself, and checked on the
            // real segment so the drawn indicator and the hit line are both covered.
            var (segA, segB) = boss.BeamSegment();
            var farOut = segA + (segB - segA).Normalized() * 5000f;
            Check(Boss.BeamLength == 10000f && Math.Abs((beam.B - beam.A).Length() - 10000f) < 1f
                  && Boss.DistToSegment(farOut, segA, segB) < 1f,
                  $"beam and indicator both reach 10000 u -- a point 5000 u out is inside it, where the old 1800 u fell short (indicator {(beam.B - beam.A).Length():0} u)");
            // Tracking, then LOCKED. Twist the hull well off the pilot first, both times: a boss
            // that is already pointing the right way looks identical whether it is turning or
            // frozen, and a check that cannot tell them apart proves nothing. 0.3 rad/s over
            // 0.5 s closes 0.15 rad, so "it moved" and "it did not" are far apart here.
            boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
            float offBy0 = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f));
            await Wait(0.5);
            float offBy1 = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f));
            Check(boss.BeamCharging && !me2.Pinned && !boss.BeamAimLocked && offBy0 - offBy1 > 0.1f,
                  $"while the pilot is free the boss keeps turning onto it ({offBy0:0.000} -> {offBy1:0.000} rad off in 0.5 s)");
            // The web lands and the aim is final: the escorts existed to stop the pilot, and the
            // moment they have, the boss stops too. The red line it is showing is the line it
            // fires. The pilot's last chance to leave the arc is BEFORE the web, not after.
            boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
            me2.PinFor(2.0);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);   // the frame the lock latches
            float heldAt = boss.Rotation;
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(boss.BeamCharging && me2.Pinned && boss.BeamAimLocked
                  && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt)) < 1e-4f,
                  $"once the pilot is webbed the boss stops turning: the aim is final (turned {Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt):0.0000} rad in 0.5 s)");
            // The two twists were a probe, and the aim is now locked, so put it back on the pilot by
            // hand: the beam below has to be the real shot, and the damage check at the end of this
            // scenario is the only thing that proves the locked line still lands.
            boss.Rotation = (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f;
            int down0 = Torpedo.Intercepted;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && Torpedo.Intercepted == down0; i++)
            {
                if (!Me.PdActive && Me.PdReady) Key(global::Godot.Key.Q);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Torpedo.Intercepted > down0, "point defence shoots the boss's missiles down");
            Check(!H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort), "point defence, switched on, kills the beam's escorts");
            var aMissile = Nodes<Torpedo>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.HostileFire);
            Check(Turret.PdPriority(boss) == 2 && (aMissile == null || Turret.PdPriority(aMissile) == 0), "point defence takes missiles before anything else");
            var tiny = new TinyTarget();
            Check(Turret.PdPriority(tiny) == 1 && Turret.PdPriority(tiny) < Turret.PdPriority(boss), "then small craft (fighter-sized), then everything else");
            Telegraph ring = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 18 && ring == null; i++) { ring = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.Line); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(ring != null && ring.Radius == Boss.WaveRadius, "the shockwave is telegraphed: a red ring before it lands");
            // without point defence, a pair of escorts reaches the pilot and pins it inside the charge
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Me.PdActive; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            boss.LaunchEscorts(me2);
            bool pinnedInTime = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < (int)(60 * Boss.BeamWindup) && !pinnedInTime; i++) { pinnedInTime = me2.Pinned; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(pinnedInTime, "left alone (no point defence), the escorts pin the pilot before the charge ends");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 25 && (tCharge < 0 || boss.Charging || tVolley < 0); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            GD.Print($"  boss rhythm: beam {tBeam:0.0} s, trident {tVolley:0.0} s, charge {tCharge:0.0} s; trident of {trident}, spread {Mathf.RadToDeg(spread):0} deg");
            // the watcher starts a moment after the boss's clock, so compare the gaps, not the times
            Check(tBeam > 0 && Math.Abs(tCharge - tBeam - 15) < 0.4 && Math.Abs(tVolley - tBeam - 7.5) < 0.4,
                  "the rhythm: the charge 15 s after the beam, a trident half-way between");
            Check(trident == 3 && Math.Abs(Mathf.RadToDeg(spread) - 50) < 3, "a trident: three double-size missiles, the outer two 25 degrees off the middle");
            double beamDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:beam", out var bd) ? bd : 0, chargeDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:charge", out var cd) ? cd : 0;
            // live 3 s, checked every 0.25 s for 50 -- one source lands at most once per 0.52 s, and
            // ticks fall on frames, so two ticks are sometimes 0.533 s apart: 5 to 7 hits of 50
            Check(beamDmg >= 200 && beamDmg <= 350 && Math.Abs(beamDmg % 50) < 1e-6, $"the beam, live 3 s at 50 a tick, through the 0.52 s guard: {beamDmg / 50:0} hits ({beamDmg:0})");
            Check(Math.Abs(chargeDmg - 40) < 1e-6, $"the charge rams for 40 ({chargeDmg:0})");
            Check(Music.I == null || Math.Abs(Boss.TridentSpeed - 135f) < 1e-3, "trident missiles fly at 135 u/s (10% slower)");
            // the kill: shared EXP for it and the mission, the bounty's credits, then home
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            boss.TakeDamage(boss.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(H.MissionWon && Character.Level == 1 && Character.Exp == 550, $"the kill of a level-1 boss pays a level-1 pilot 200 + 250 first clear + 100 = 550 (exp {Character.Exp})");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && H.Yard != null, "home again after the win");
            await Wait(Hub.RaidDelay + 0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "a won mission brings no raid");
            // exactly the credits set aside at departure + the 2000 bounty (a hauler sale before
            // departure is part of what was set aside, so a fixed window was fragile)
            Check(Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - Yard.TripStartCredits - 2000) < 1e-6, $"the bounty's 2000 credits are paid at home ({Yard.TripStartCredits:0} + 2000 = {H.Yard.Credits:0})");
            // the literal 1/20 asked for -- not Yard.AwayShare, or the check could never fail
            double want = H.Yard.FleetRate(GatherKind.Miner) * Yard.LastAway / 20.0;
            Check(Yard.LastAway > 1 && Yard.LastAwayOre > 0 && Math.Abs(Yard.LastAwayOre - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"while away the base earned 1/20 of its rate ({Yard.LastAwayOre:0.0} ore for {Yard.LastAway:0} s)");
            // levels: the win cleared level 1; docking at the TIO selects level 2
            Check(Character.BossCleared.TryGetValue("silver_lancer", out var cleared) && cleared.Contains(1), "the win records level 1 as cleared");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.S(2) - 1.1) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(3) - 1.21) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.Current.Hull * Missions.S(2) - 660) < 1e-6,
                  "each level is 10% stronger, multiplicatively (level 2: 660 hull)");
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1);
            var tw2 = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "docking at the TIO selects the newest unlocked level (2)");
            Check(Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").Disabled, "no level beyond the newest unlocked");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelDown").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 1, "the host can pick an easier, cleared level");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "and back up to the newest");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            // a wipe: every ship in stasis at once -> the mission fails, home with nothing
            H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Arena); await Wait(1.5);
            int exp1 = Character.Exp; int failLevel = Missions.Level;
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Character.Exp == exp1 && Me.Alive, "the whole party in stasis: the mission fails, home with no reward");
            // ...and the boss sends its raiders after you: 2 patrols (solo), from the map's edge, at the failed level's strength
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "the raid is not there at once (every guest's world loads first)");
            // measured the frame they appear: a raider that spots a miner boosts inward at once
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && H.Raiders.Count == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double sL = Math.Pow(1.1, failLevel - 1);
            var raid = H.Raiders.ToList();
            GD.Print($"  raid: {raid.Count} raiders, level {failLevel} (x{sL:0.00}), {raid.Min(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0}-{raid.Max(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0} u out");
            Check(raid.Count == 8 && raid.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 2 && raid.Select(r => r.Patrol).Distinct().Count() == 2,
                  "a failed mission: the boss sends 2 patrols (3 lights and a heavy each)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - Hub.RaidEdge) < 110f), "in from the edge of the base's map (3200 u out)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - sL) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - (r.Heavy ? 100 : 25) * sL) < 1e-6),
                  $"at the failed boss's level: hull and damage x{sL:0.00} (1.1^(L-1), L = {failLevel})");
            foreach (var r in raid) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
            await Wait(0.3);
            // the fighter-class autopilot, flown on its own (no player fighter class yet)
            Vector2 fp = Vector2.Zero, fv = Vector2.Zero; float fh = 0f; var goal = new Vector2(800, -600);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12; i++)
            {
                var (turn, thrust) = Autopilot.Fighter(fp, fh, fv, goal, 300f, 30f);
                fh += turn * 4f / 60f; fv += Vector2.Right.Rotated(fh) * thrust * 600f / 60f; fv *= 0.985f;
                if (fv.Length() > 300f) fv = fv.Normalized() * 300f;
                fp += fv / 60f;
            }
            Check(fp.DistanceTo(goal) < 80f, $"the fighter-class autopilot reaches its point ({fp.DistanceTo(goal):0} u off)");
            Check(Settings.MusicOn, "music is on by default");
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.Save();
            Me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 330); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Nodes<BasePanel>().Any(), "left-click on the base station opens the BASE menu");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        }

        // ── radar and the Esc menu ──
        {
            var radar = Nodes<Radar>().First();
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[Settings.RadarSize]), "the radar sits top right at the chosen size");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.EscMenuOpen && Hub.ControlsLocked, "Esc (nothing else open) opens the menu; the helm is locked");
            var menu = Nodes<EscMenu>().First();
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "LARGE").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Settings.RadarSize == 2 && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[2]), "LARGE radar: saved and applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "SMALL").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[0]), "SMALL radar applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "MEDIUM").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "50%").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(Settings.MusicVolume, 0.5f), "music volume set from the menu");
            // ...and the row LIGHTS the live step. The default used to be 0.6, which is not one of
            // the five the menu offers, so the match that picks the lit button found nothing and
            // the row opened with none of them pressed. Both rows are checked: a settings row where
            // nothing is selected looks broken whichever row it is.
            foreach (var g in new[] { "Radar", "Music" })
            {
                var opts = Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(menu).First(r => r.Name == g)).ToList();
                Check(opts.Count > 1 && opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed) == 1,
                      $"the {g} row shows exactly one live choice ({opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed)} of {opts.Count} lit)");
            }
            // ...and the harness cap SURVIVES the menu re-applying the volume. Checked here, after
            // a menu has just written the audio settings, rather than at startup where nothing has
            // had a chance to overwrite it. 5% as a literal, never Settings.TestVolume: a check
            // that reads the value it is testing cannot catch the value being wrong.
            int mbus = AudioServer.GetBusIndex("Master");
            float lin = mbus < 0 ? -1f : (float)Mathf.DbToLinear(AudioServer.GetBusVolumeDb(mbus));
            Check(mbus >= 0 && !AudioServer.IsBusMute(mbus) && Mathf.Abs(lin - 0.05f) < 0.001f && Settings.Volume > 0.05f,
                  $"the harness runs at 5% master volume whatever the settings say ({lin * 100:0.0}%, setting {Settings.Volume * 100:0}%)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!H.EscMenuOpen, "Esc closes the menu");
        }

        // ── music by mood: ambient, 20% combat on a selected enemy, 100% in combat ──
        {
            var sh = Me; var mus = Music.I;
            Check(mus != null, "the music player is running");
            H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
            await Wait(9.0);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.AmbientLevel, 1f) && mus.CombatLevel == 0f, "nothing selected: ambient only");
            H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"));
            await Wait(2.5);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Alert && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 0.2f), $"enemy selected: combat music at 20% ({mus.CombatLevel:0.00})");
            Check(Math.Abs(Music.I.AmbientLevel - 0.35f) < 0.02, $"and the ambient at half its alert level under it (0.35: {Music.I.AmbientLevel:0.00})");
            sh.Hit(1, sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * 80f); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Combat && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 1f) && mus.AmbientLevel == 0f,
                  "taking damage: combat music at 100%");
            H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
            await Wait(PlayerShip.CombatHold + 3.0);
            Check(!sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient, "8 s after the last damage, back to ambient");
            sh.TakeDamage(-1);
        }

        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 0 && H != null, "Esc closes the K window first");
        H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Nodes<Button>(Nodes<EscMenu>().First()).First(b => b.Name == "Quit").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.3);
        Check(GetTree().CurrentScene is MainMenu, "QUIT TO MAIN MENU returns to the main menu");
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(0.4);
        Check(H != null && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, "hub re-enters cleanly");

        // ── sound: the music 35% quieter; effects by the camera's distance and zoom ──
        Check(Math.Abs(Music.Master - 0.65f) < 1e-6, "the music plays 35% quieter than it started");
        Check(Math.Abs(Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1)) < 1e-4 && Sfx.VolumeAt(1000, 1) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3
              && Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 0.5f) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3 && float.IsNegativeInfinity(Sfx.VolumeAt(6000, 1)),
              "effects: 0 dB at the view's centre, quieter with distance and when zoomed out, silent far away");
        Check(new[] { "laser_light", "laser_boss", "laser_hit", "missile_whoosh", "impact_thunk" }.All(n => GD.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{n}.wav") != null),
              "all five effects load");
        int P(string n) => Sfx.Played.TryGetValue(n, out var v) ? v : 0;
        GD.Print($"  sounds played: light laser {P("laser_light")}, boss laser {P("laser_boss")}, hit {P("laser_hit")}, whoosh {P("missile_whoosh")}, thunk {P("impact_thunk")}");
        Check(P("laser_light") > 0 && P("laser_hit") > 0, "lasers buzz where they fire and crackle where they hit");
        Check(P("laser_boss") > 0, "the boss's lasers have their own, deeper buzz");
        Check(P("missile_whoosh") > 0 && P("impact_thunk") > 0, "missiles and torpedoes whoosh at launch and thunk when they hit");

        // leaks: leaving and re-entering the hub must not grow the orphan-node or object
        // counts (the first cycle warms caches; compare the next two against it)
        async Task Cycle()
        {
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://MainMenu.tscn"); await Wait(0.5);
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(1.0);
        }
        // Collect .NET garbage first, so the count shows real leaks, not objects merely
        // waiting for the collector (it runs whenever it likes).
        async Task<double> Settled()
        {
            for (int g = 0; g < 2; g++) { System.GC.Collect(); System.GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers(); }
            for (int f = 0; f < 3; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            return Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectCount);
        }
        await Cycle();
        double objA = await Settled(), orphA = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        await Cycle(); await Cycle();
        double objB = await Settled(), orphB = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        GD.Print($"  leak check: orphan nodes {orphA:0} -> {orphB:0}; objects {objA:0} -> {objB:0} (after collection)");
        Check(orphB <= orphA, "no orphan nodes accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
        Check(objB <= objA + 20, "no objects accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
    }

    // ── multiplayer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // ── a dedicated two-player arena run (its own port, after the three-player run) ──
    // Mission 1: both READY, both at the portal, into the arena together; the boss dies;
    // both home. Mission 2: into the arena again; the host drops -- the guest must land
    // home, offline, with its OWN base (a marker credit total set before it joined).
    async Task ArenaMp(string role)
    {
        const int Port = 27125;
        async Task ToArena(string who, int mission)
        {
            // the home world must be BUILT first: the scene swap is deferred, and for a frame
            // or two the old one is still current
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && (H == null || Hub.InArena || H.GetNodeOrNull("TIO") == null || !H.IsNodeReady()); i++)
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            H.SetMyReady(true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && H.Mission != Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && !Hub.InArena; i++)
            {
                if (H != null && Me != null && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen) { Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"{who}: into the arena with the other pilot (mission {mission})");
        }
        if (role == "ahost")
        {
